# Revisi L — Pajak Indonesia 2026 (PPh OP, TER, pajak investasi, UMKM, PPN, ringkasan tahunan) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The client asked: "fetch berapa biaya pajak di Indonesia di masa Presiden Prabowo ini dan implement ke website." This plan makes WealthMe's tax features match the rules in force in 2024–2026. It covers five things, all decided by the user:

- (a) an accurate annual PPh Orang Pribadi: full PTKP status, biaya jabatan, iuran pensiun/JHT, zakat, NPPN for freelancers, and the Pasal 17 brackets;
- (b) PPh 21 TER monthly withholding with the December true-up and the PPh 21 DTP incentive flag;
- (c) automatic final-tax estimates per investment instrument, shown on /investasi, /rekening and in /laporan;
- (d) a UMKM 0,5 % final-tax calculator plus PPN information in the transaction context (information only, no ledger change);
- (e) an annual summary per tax year: PPh OP + final taxes + UMKM + SPT status and deadline.

**Architecture:**

- **One rule registry.** Every rate, threshold, table and deadline lives in `apps/api/src/tax-rules/` as effective-dated entries `{ key, value, effectiveFrom, effectiveTo, legalBasis, source, verified, note }`. `ruleAt(key, date)` picks the entry in force on a WIB calendar date. Nothing else in the codebase holds a tax number (a guard spec enforces this for the tax files).
- **Pure BigInt engines.** `apps/api/src/tax-engine/` holds pure functions: annual PPh OP, TER + December true-up, DTP eligibility, final tax per instrument, UMKM, PPN, SPT deadline. All money is BigInt; rates are integer basis points; rounding is explicit (PKP floor to Rp 1.000 as today, everything else round-half-up).
- **Legacy parity.** Existing `TaxRecord` rows get `calcMode = legacy` by default and keep computing exactly as before, now through the BigInt engine. A characterization spec written against today's code **before** the refactor pins 14 golden rows and must stay green.
- **Server-authoritative.** Every number the web shows comes from an API response. Every response carries `rules: TaxRuleRefDto[]`, so the UI can label unverified numbers with "perkiraan — cek aturan terbaru DJP" and a source link.
- **Storage (additive).** New nullable columns on `tax_records` (detailed mode), a new `tax_final_income_entries` table (manual dividend/rent/savings/P2P/gold lines), a new `tax_umkm_years` table (monthly omzet), and a nullable `investments.cryptoExchange`.

**Tech Stack:**

- API: NestJS 11, Prisma 6.19 (PostgreSQL), class-validator/class-transformer, Jest + ts-jest, supertest.
- Web: Next.js 16 App Router, React 19, TanStack Query, Jest + Testing Library.
- Shared types: `@wealthme/contracts`. Workspace: pnpm.

**Spec / research:** The client requirement quoted in the Goal, the user decisions (a)–(e), and `docs/research/2026-09-26-pajak-indonesia.md`. Read the research fully before Task 1. Every number that comes from it is cited as **[R§n]** (section number) or **[R-YAML]** (its machine-readable block). Numbers the research does **not** contain are marked **[not in R]** and always ship as `verified: false`.

## Global Constraints

- **Workspace.** Work only in `/home/wit/WORK/vibes/wealthme-web-revisi-g` on branch `feat/revisi-i-multi-currency`. Never touch `/home/wit/WORK/vibes/wealthme-web`.
- **Concurrent agents.** Other agents are editing the web holdings pages (`aset`, `investasi`, `laporan/_components/laporan-inner.tsx`, `laporan/_components/investment-dialogs.tsx`) and possibly sub-plan K files on this branch.
  - Before every task run `git status` and `git log -1`.
  - Stage only the files your task names (`git add <paths>`, never `git add -A` / `git add .`).
  - Task 11 mounts one line in `investasi/page.tsx`, `rekening/page.tsx` and `transaksi/page.tsx`. If a file you must mount into has uncommitted changes that are not yours, **do not edit it**: finish the rest of the task and report the one-line mount as pending.
- **Protected files (never edit):** `apps/web/src/app/globals.css`, `apps/web/src/app/design-system.css`, `apps/web/src/lib/*`, `apps/web/src/components/ui/*`, `apps/web/src/components/wm/*`, `apps/web/src/components/charts/*`, `apps/web/src/components/shell/*`, `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`, `packages/config/*`. Query keys therefore live next to their hooks (`apps/web/src/components/tax/tax-query-keys.ts`), not in `@/lib/query-keys`.
- **i18n.** Every new user-visible string goes into **all three** of `apps/web/src/i18n/types.ts`, `id.ts`, `en.ts` under `tax.*` (dictionaries use `satisfies I18nDictionary`).
- **Controllers.** DTO classes used by `@Body()`/`@Query()` and injected services are value imports preceded by `// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/consistent-type-imports`.
- **Never run `eslint --fix` on an API service, controller or module file.** It rewrites DI imports to `import type` and breaks Nest boot while specs stay green. Husky's pre-commit runs `eslint --fix` on staged files: after each API commit run `git show HEAD -- <controller/service>` and confirm the value imports survived; if not, fix by hand in a follow-up commit.
- **Money.** BigInt on the API, digit strings on the wire (`^\d{1,15}$`, the existing `MAX_TAX_DIGITS` cap). Rates are integer basis points (`1 bps = 0,01 %`). Never `Number`/`parseFloat` in new API math. The only float path left is the existing public `POST /calculators/pph21` (it accepts float `IsNumber` input and must keep its response byte-identical).
- **Rounding.**
  - PKP is floored to the Rp 1.000 unit from the `pph.pkpRounding` rule (existing behaviour, `roundPkpDown`; the research does not state it — **[not in R]**, `verified: false`).
  - Pasal 17 tax on a Rp 1.000-multiple PKP is always an integer (5/15/25/30/35 % of a multiple of 1.000), so no rounding happens there.
  - Biaya jabatan is kept exact in hundredths of a rupiah internally (legacy parity: `gross × 5 %` can be fractional). It is exposed rounded half-up to the rupiah for display only.
  - Every other amount (TER withholding, final taxes, UMKM, PPN, accrued interest) is round-half-up to the rupiah via `divHalfUp`.
- **Migrations.** Additive only. The folder must sort after the latest one on the branch: re-run `ls apps/api/prisma/migrations` at the start of Task 5; the plan uses `20260926180000_revisi_l_tax`, bump it if K (or anything else) has landed a later one. Never hand-apply DDL; after the schema change run `pnpm exec prisma generate` in `apps/api`.
- **Contracts.** `packages/contracts/dist` is committed. After editing `packages/contracts/src`, run `pnpm --filter @wealthme/contracts build` and `git add packages/contracts/dist`.
- **Sub-plan I (fxInputs) compatibility.** Every new money input in this plan is **IDR-only by design**, with no `FX_FIELD_SPECS` entry. Why: Indonesian tax is assessed in rupiah, and foreign income must be converted at the Minister of Finance's weekly _kurs pajak_ (KMK), not the market rate that `fxInputs` applies. Converting with `fxInputs` would silently produce a wrong tax base. Holdings in a non-IDR currency are skipped by the estimator with an explicit `skippedForeign` count, never converted.
- **Tenant scoping.** Every family-scoped route has `@PageAccess(...)` and `@CheckAbility(...)`, and every Prisma query filters by `familyGroupId`. Foreign keys supplied by the client (`investmentId`, `accountId`) are verified with `findFirst({ where: { id, familyGroupId } })` before use (400 otherwise). The static-law endpoints `GET /calculators/tax-rules` and `POST /calculators/ppn` are `@Public()` under the existing public calculators controller: they read no family data, exactly like the existing `POST /calculators/pph21`, and the global `ThrottlerGuard` covers them.
- **Honest UI.** Any number whose rule is `verified: false`, or resolved `outOfRange`, shows the badge "perkiraan — cek aturan terbaru DJP" with a link to the rule's `source`. Rules with no rate at all (`rateBps: null`: emas, P2P) show "tarif belum terverifikasi — isi sendiri" and a user-editable rate. Nothing is presented as an official filing figure.
- **Tests.** Run only the focused jest commands each task names. No `next build`, no full turbo runs.
- **Lint.** Every touched file must pass `pnpm exec eslint <files>` (no `--fix`) from its owning app directory. `pnpm exec tsc --noEmit -p .` in that app must gain no new errors.
- **Commits.** Conventional Commits. End each message with:
  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01RDbXyuMbb2WHtT74hdL9Zy
  ```
  Never `--no-verify`. The post-commit hook may print "post-commit script failed (code 1)" (graphify noise); the commit still lands.
- **Comments.** None unless the WHY is non-obvious; one line max.
- **UI tasks (9, 10, 11).** Invoke `taste-skill:taste-skill` before writing TSX and follow its pre-flight plus `~/.claude/rules/ecc/web/design-quality.md`. Reuse the page's existing classes (`.field`, `.label`, `.input`, `.select`, `.hint`, `.notice notice--info|warning`, `.badge badge--info|warning`, `.btn btn--outline|ghost btn--sm`, `.table table--num`) and the `@/components/wm` primitives (`SectionCard`, `StatusBadge`, `HeroCard`). Add no new CSS files. Screenshots: `npx -y @playwright/cli` against the running dev web (:3000), PNGs under `.superpowers/sdd/2026-09-26-revisi-l-pajak-2026/shots/`; the real-data sweep account opens pages but never submits.
- **Token thrift.** Wrap noisy commands with `rtk` (`rtk test pnpm exec jest <path>`, `rtk err pnpm exec tsc --noEmit -p .`).

## Key design decisions (read before coding)

1. **One registry, effective-dated, selected by WIB calendar date.**
   - `ruleAt(key, "YYYY-MM-DD")` returns the entry whose `[effectiveFrom, effectiveTo]` (both inclusive; `effectiveTo: null` = open) contains the date.
   - Annual rules (Pasal 17, PTKP, biaya jabatan, zakat, PKP rounding) are selected at **31 Dec of the tax year**. Monthly rules (TER, DTP, UMKM) at the **1st of the month**. Transaction rules (final taxes, PPN) at the **transaction's WIB date**.
   - A date before a key's earliest entry resolves to that earliest entry with `outOfRange: true`. This is what keeps legacy records unchanged: a tax year 2019 record still uses the UU HPP brackets it always used (the pre-2022 50/250/500 jt structure is **not** modelled), and the UI labels it "perkiraan". A date after the last closed entry with no open entry throws (a data bug; the registry spec forbids it).
   - The registry spec forbids overlapping entries per key, gaps between entries of a key, and any key without an open-ended (`effectiveTo: null`) last entry.
2. **Legacy records are untouched.** `tax_records.calcMode` defaults to `legacy`. Legacy rows compute `netTax` from `(grossIncome, ptkp, zakat-from-donations)` exactly as today: biaya jabatan 5 % capped Rp 6 jt, no pension, PKP floored to Rp 1.000, Pasal 17.
   - The formula moves to the BigInt engine. Parity is proved three ways:
     - a characterization spec (Task 2, Step 1) written against **today's** `computeNetTax` before any refactor, 14 fixture rows including a fractional biaya jabatan (Rp 61.234.567) and the 15-digit ceiling;
     - the existing `pph21.util.spec.ts` / `calculators.service.spec.ts` goldens stay untouched and green;
     - an exhaustive check (run while planning) of every gross that is a multiple of Rp 20 up to Rp 200 jt, where float `gross * 0.05` equals the exact value. Non-multiples give a non-integer PKP-before-floor that float error (< 1e-6) cannot push across a Rp 1.000 boundary.
   - A record becomes `detailed` only when a write sends `maritalStatus`. A PATCH that changes only `sptStatus` keeps a legacy row legacy.
3. **`PERPULUHAN_RATE` stays where it is.** It is a religious tithe (10 % of income by the user's faith), not a statutory tax: it has no legal basis, no effective date and no DJP source, so it does not belong in the tax registry. The same applies to `NISAB_GRAMS` / `INDICATIVE_GOLD_PRICE_PER_GRAM` (zakat maal). Only zakat's **tax-deductibility** is a tax rule (`pph.zakat`).
4. **PTKP status model.** `maritalStatus ∈ {TK, K, KI}` × `dependents ∈ 0..3`. PTKP = base Rp 54 jt + (K or KI: Rp 4,5 jt) + (KI: Rp 54 jt for the wife's combined income) + dependents × Rp 4,5 jt **[R§3]**. So there are 12 combinations, from TK/0 Rp 54.000.000 to K/I/3 Rp 126.000.000.
   - TER category mapping comes from **[R§1]** (verified): A = TK/0, TK/1, K/0; B = TK/2, TK/3, K/1, K/2; C = K/3.
   - K/I is an **annual SPT** status; the employer withholds on the husband's own K/n status, so TER maps K/I/n → the K/n category. **[not in R]** — shown with the unverified badge on the TER panel when K/I is picked.
5. **TER tables.** The research gives only the category endpoints (A: 0 % up to Rp 5,4 jt, 34 % above Rp 1,4 M; C: 0 % up to Rp 6,6 jt, 34 % above Rp 1,419 M; B: ~40 bands) **[R§1]**. The full band tables below are transcribed from the PP 58/2023 Lampiran and match every endpoint the research gives. They are still **[not in R]** at band level, so `pph21.terTables` ships `verified: false` with the PMK 168/2023 PDF as its source. Band `upTo` values are inclusive ("s.d."); a monthly gross of exactly Rp 5.400.000 is 0 %, and Rp 5.400.001 is 0,25 %.
6. **December true-up.** The December withholding is the annual Pasal 17 tax on the full year (gross Jan–Dec − biaya jabatan − employee pension/JHT/JP − zakat − PTKP, PKP floored) minus the TER withholding Jan–Nov **[R§1]**. It can be negative (lebih potong → refund in December payroll); the result keeps the sign and sets `isRefund`. December's own TER row is not computed (TER applies to Jan–Nov only).
7. **PPh 21 DTP.** Eligibility is per month, from the `pph21.dtp` rule in force on the 1st of that month **[R§1, R-YAML]**:
   - The sector must be covered.
   - Permanent employees need a monthly gross ≤ Rp 10.000.000 (**inclusive**: exactly Rp 10 jt is eligible).
   - Non-permanent employees need a daily wage ≤ Rp 500.000, or, from the 2026 rule, a monthly gross ≤ Rp 10 jt.
   - 2026 also requires a Coretax-validated NIK.
   - 2024 has no program. The 2025 tourism extension (PMK 72/2025, "~Oct 2025") is modelled from 2025-10-01 with `verified: false`, because the month is approximate.
   - When eligible, the DTP amount is that month's TER withholding (the employer pays it in cash; take-home is not reduced). The December row's DTP uses `max(december, 0)` and is always labelled unverified (the research does not say how DTP treats the true-up).
8. **Zakat.** Deductible only when paid through BAZNAS/official LAZ **[R§4]**. The existing donations rule (`TAX_DEDUCTIBLE_DONATION_TYPES` + `isTaxDeductible`) already enforces that for saved records.
   - Cap: `zakatApplied = min(zakat, neto)`. Zakat can reduce neto to 0 but never below, and never offsets PTKP into a "negative PKP" carried elsewhere. This is equivalent to today's `max(0, …)` (proved by the legacy parity rows `50 jt gross, 100 jt zakat → 0`).
   - The research gives no other cap (`pph.zakat` ships `verified: false`).
9. **NPPN.** The PER-17/PJ/2015 percentage table was **not retrieved** **[R§4]**. So NPPN is "user enters the NPPN %" (1–100 %, as bps), labelled unverified, with the PDF linked. Freelance income under the UMKM 0,5 % final regime must **not** also be entered as NPPN; the UI says so.
10. **Final taxes on investments** **[R§6]**. Each instrument is one registry key with `rateBps` (or `null` when unknown).
    - **Automatic** lines are derived from holdings the family already records:
      - deposito: accrued interest in the tax year → 20 % (principal ≤ Rp 7,5 jt → exempt, `verified: false`);
      - obligasi: accrued coupon in the tax year → treated as SBN, 10 % (`verified: false`, labelled "diasumsikan SBN");
      - saham: sell value in the year → 0,1 %;
      - kripto: sell value → 0,21 % domestic / 1 % foreign from 2025-08-01. Before that date, PMK 68/2022's 0,1 % / 0,2 % **[not in R]** apply, `verified: false`. The exchange comes from the new `investments.cryptoExchange`; null means "domestik", shown as an assumption;
      - reksa dana: sells → 0 at investor level (`verified: false`);
      - emas: sells → rate `null` (research gap).
    - **Manual** lines (`tax_final_income_entries`) cover what the app has no data for:
      - dividen: 10 %, or 0 % when the user ticks "diinvestasikan kembali ≤ 3 tahun";
      - bunga tabungan: 20 %;
      - sewa: 10 %;
      - P2P: rate `null`;
      - emas: rate `null`;
      - kupon SBN received outside a tracked holding: 10 %;
      - bunga deposito outside a tracked holding: 20 %.
    - Every line may carry `rateBpsOverride` (0–10000). A line whose rate is `null` and has no override shows tax "—" and counts in `missingRateCount`.
    - Interest accrual uses days `[max(purchasedAt, 1 Jan), min(maturityDate, 1 Jan next))` (half-open, WIB dates) / 365, round-half-up. A missing `purchasedAt` accrues from 1 Jan and says so. Founder-share surcharge (0,5 %) is out of scope.
11. **UMKM** **[R§5]**.
    - 0,5 % per month on omzet. For individuals, cumulative omzet up to Rp 500 jt per year is exempt; the month that crosses it pays on the excess.
    - `exceedsCeiling` when the cumulative total is > Rp 4,8 M. That month and later ones are still 0,5 % this year, but the flag says next year must use Pasal 17 with bookkeeping.
    - Time limit: until 2026-04-21, individuals had 7 tax years from `firstRegisteredYear` (PP 55/2022 Pasal 59). From 2026-04-22 (PP 20/2026) there is no limit, so a month is ineligible only if the rule in force on its 1st day has a limit and `taxYear ≥ firstRegisteredYear + 7`. Ineligible months show tax `null` + reason `time_limit` ("gunakan tarif Pasal 17").
    - Both entries ship `verified: false` (the YAML block marks them so).
12. **PPN** **[R§7]**. The rate is 12 % nominal. The DPP is 11/12 for non-luxury goods, which gives an effective 11 %. Luxury goods use DPP 12/12 from 2025-02-01; January 2025 luxury still used 11/12. Before 2025 the rate was 11 % on DPP 1/1 (**[not in R]** as a figure, `verified: false`).
    - Two modes: price excluding PPN → `ppn = divHalfUp(price × num × nominalBps, den × 10000)`; price including PPN → `base = divHalfUp(price × den × 10000, den × 10000 + num × nominalBps)`, `ppn = price − base`.
    - Information only: the PPN hint on /transaksi never changes an amount or a ledger row.
13. **SPT.** Deadline 31 March of `taxYear + 1` **[R§10]**. Grace (penalty waiver, not a deadline change) is from `filing.sptGrace` at that date: 2026-04-30 for tax year 2025 **[R§10]** (verified); `null` otherwise.
    - The summary reports `deadline`, `graceUntil`, `daysToDeadline` (WIB today), `pastDeadline` and `withinGrace`.
    - It **never** rewrites the stored `sptStatus`.
    - `sptFiledAt` is stamped server-side when a write moves `sptStatus` to `sudah_lapor`.
    - Coretax is shown as labelled info text only.

## Review Focus

1. **Bracket boundaries.** PKP Rp 60.000.000 → Rp 3.000.000. Rp 60.001.000 → Rp 3.000.150. Rp 250.000.000 → Rp 31.500.000. Rp 500.000.000 → Rp 94.000.000. Rp 5.000.000.000 → Rp 1.444.000.000. Rp 5.000.001.000 → Rp 1.444.000.350.
2. **PTKP combos.** All 12: TK/0–3 = 54 / 58,5 / 63 / 67,5 jt. K/0–3 = 58,5 / 63 / 67,5 / 72 jt. K/I/0–3 = 112,5 / 117 / 121,5 / 126 jt. `dependents = 4` is rejected (400), never silently capped.
3. **TER edges.**

   | Category | Monthly gross | Rate   | Withholding |
   | -------- | ------------- | ------ | ----------- |
   | A        | 5.400.000     | 0      | 0           |
   | A        | 5.400.001     | 25 bps | 13.500      |
   | A        | 1.400.000.000 | 3300   | 462.000.000 |
   | A        | 1.400.000.001 | 3400   | 476.000.000 |
   | B        | 6.200.000     | 0      | 0           |
   | B        | 6.200.001     | 25     | 15.500      |
   | B        | 1.405.000.000 | 3300   | 463.650.000 |
   | B        | 1.405.000.001 | 3400   | 477.700.000 |
   | C        | 6.600.000     | 0      | 0           |
   | C        | 6.600.001     | 25     | 16.500      |
   | C        | 1.419.000.000 | 3300   | 468.270.000 |
   | C        | 1.419.000.001 | 3400   | 482.460.000 |

4. **December true-up.**
   - K/1, Rp 10 jt × 12 → TER B 150 bps = Rp 150.000/month, Jan–Nov Rp 1.650.000, annual Rp 2.550.000, December **Rp 900.000**. With Rp 3,6 jt employee pension the annual is Rp 2.370.000 and December **Rp 720.000**.
   - Refund case: TK/0, January Rp 50 jt (bonus; TER A 1800 bps = Rp 9.000.000) then Rp 10 jt/month (TER A 200 bps = Rp 200.000). Jan–Nov = Rp 11.000.000; annual on Rp 160 jt = PKP Rp 100 jt → Rp 9.000.000; December **−Rp 2.000.000**, `isRefund: true`.
5. **Zakat cap.** Gross Rp 50 jt, TK/0, zakat Rp 100 jt → zakatApplied = neto Rp 47.500.000, PKP 0, tax 0. Legacy row `(50 jt, 54 jt, 100 jt)` → 0 as before.
6. **DTP edges.**
   - Rp 10.000.000 permanent, tekstil_pakaian, 2026-03, NIK validated → eligible, DTP = TER (TK/0: Rp 200.000; K/3: Rp 150.000).
   - Rp 10.000.001 → `gross_above_cap`. 2026 without a validated NIK → `nik_not_validated`. 2024 → `no_program`.
   - pariwisata 2025-09 → `sector_not_covered`; 2025-10 → eligible (`verified: false`).
7. **Effective-date switching.**
   - kripto domestik sale of Rp 50 jt on 2025-07-31 → 10 bps Rp 50.000; on 2025-08-01 → 21 bps Rp 105.000; foreign on 2025-08-01 → Rp 500.000.
   - PPN on Rp 1 jt, 2024-12-31 → Rp 110.000 (DPP 1/1, 11 %). Luxury 2025-01-15 → Rp 110.000. Luxury 2025-02-01 → Rp 120.000. Non-luxury 2026 → Rp 110.000.
   - UMKM with firstRegisteredYear 2018: tax year 2025 → all months ineligible. 2026 → Jan–Apr ineligible (rule at 1 Apr still has the limit), May–Dec eligible.
   - Tax year 2019 record → HPP brackets with `outOfRange: true`.
8. **Rounding.**
   - Biaya jabatan on Rp 61.234.567 = Rp 3.061.728,35 kept exact → PKP 4.172.000 → tax 208.600 (legacy identical).
   - Tabungan Rp 1.234.567 × 20 % = Rp 246.913. UMKM excess Rp 123.456.789 × 0,5 % = Rp 617.284. PPN Rp 999.999 non-lux = Rp 110.000.
   - Deposito Rp 100 jt @ 4,25 % from 2026-03-01 → 306 days → interest Rp 3.563.014 → tax Rp 712.603.
9. **Unverified-label honesty.** Every response's `rules[]` includes each rule actually used, and the web badge appears whenever any used rule is `verified: false` or `outOfRange`. Task 9–11 tests assert the badge text and the `source` href.
10. **Existing records unchanged.** The characterization spec rows, `pph21.util.spec.ts` and `calculators.service.spec.ts` stay green. `GET /tax` on a legacy row returns the same `netTax` as before, plus the new fields as `null` and `calcMode: "legacy"`.

## Out of scope (deliberately untouched)

- Property taxes (PBB-P2, BPHTB, PPh final on land sale, PPN DTP housing) and vehicle taxes (PKB + opsen). The research covers them **[R§8–9]**, but the user decisions (a)–(e) do not ask for them. The registry shape takes them later with no engine change.
- Pension recipients' biaya pensiun, TER harian (daily-wage TER table), founder-share 0,5 % surcharge, koperasi/PT UMKM time limits, the pre-2022 Pasal 17 structure.
- Filing to DJP / Coretax integration. The app records status only.
- Changing any ledger amount. The PPN hint and the final-tax estimates are information only.

## Resolved questions (user decisions, 2026-09-26)

(a)–(e) as listed in the Goal, all binding. Unverified numbers are labelled and user-editable where it makes sense (rate overrides on final-tax lines, NPPN %, UMKM first-registered year).

```ts
import { applyBps, divHalfUp, floorTo, parseDigits } from "./money";

describe("tax money helpers", () => {
  it("divHalfUp rounds .5 up and below .5 down", () => {
    expect(divHalfUp(5n, 2n)).toBe(3n);
    expect(divHalfUp(7n, 3n)).toBe(2n);
    expect(divHalfUp(0n, 7n)).toBe(0n);
  });
  it("divHalfUp refuses negatives and zero divisors", () => {
    expect(() => divHalfUp(-1n, 2n)).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => divHalfUp(1n, 0n)).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
  it("applyBps is round-half-up on basis points", () => {
    expect(applyBps(1_234_567n, 2000)).toBe(246_913n);
    expect(applyBps(123_456_789n, 50)).toBe(617_284n);
    expect(applyBps(5_400_001n, 25)).toBe(13_500n);
  });
  it("floorTo floors positives and clamps non-positives to 0", () => {
    expect(floorTo(4_172_838n, 1000n)).toBe(4_172_000n);
    expect(floorTo(-5n, 1000n)).toBe(0n);
  });
  it("parseDigits accepts digit strings only", () => {
    expect(parseDigits("0012")).toBe(12n);
    expect(() => parseDigits("1e21")).toThrow(RangeError);
    expect(() => parseDigits("")).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `money.ts`**

```ts
export function divHalfUp(n: bigint, d: bigint): bigint {
  if (d <= 0n || n < 0n)
    throw new RangeError("divHalfUp expects n >= 0, d > 0");
  const q = n / d;
  return 2n * (n % d) >= d ? q + 1n : q;
}

export function applyBps(amount: bigint, bps: number): bigint {
  return divHalfUp(amount * BigInt(bps), 10_000n);
}

export function floorTo(amount: bigint, unit: bigint): bigint {
  return amount <= 0n ? 0n : (amount / unit) * unit;
}

export function parseDigits(s: string): bigint {
  if (!/^\d{1,18}$/.test(s)) throw new RangeError(`not a digit string: ${s}`);
  return BigInt(s);
}
```

```ts
export type TerCategory = "A" | "B" | "C";
export type DtpSector =
  | "alas_kaki"
  | "tekstil_pakaian"
  | "furnitur"
  | "kulit"
  | "pariwisata";
export interface Fraction {
  num: bigint;
  den: bigint;
}

export interface Pasal17Band {
  upTo: bigint | null;
  rateBps: number;
  label: string;
}
export interface PtkpValue {
  base: bigint;
  marriedAddOn: bigint;
  dependentAddOn: bigint;
  maxDependents: number;
  combinedWifeIncomeAddOn: bigint;
}
export interface BiayaJabatanValue {
  rateBps: number;
  capAnnual: bigint;
  capMonthly: bigint;
}
export interface PkpRoundingValue {
  unit: bigint;
}
export interface ZakatValue {
  capToNeto: true;
}
export interface NppnValue {
  table: null;
}
export interface TerBand {
  upTo: bigint | null;
  rateBps: number;
}
export type TerTablesValue = Readonly<Record<TerCategory, readonly TerBand[]>>;
export type TerCategoryMapValue = Readonly<
  Record<
    "TK" | "K",
    readonly [TerCategory, TerCategory, TerCategory, TerCategory]
  >
>;
export interface DtpValue {
  sectors: readonly DtpSector[];
  permanentMaxMonthlyGross: bigint;
  nonPermanentMaxDailyWage: bigint;
  nonPermanentMaxMonthlyGross: bigint | null;
  requiresValidatedNik: boolean;
}
export interface FinalRateValue {
  rateBps: number | null;
  minPrincipal: bigint | null;
}
export interface DividendValue {
  notReinvestedBps: number;
  reinvestedBps: number;
  reinvestYears: number;
}
export interface IdxShareValue {
  rateBps: number;
  founderSurchargeBps: number;
}
export interface UmkmValue {
  rateBps: number;
  individualExemption: bigint;
  turnoverCeiling: bigint;
  individualTimeLimitYears: number | null;
}
export interface PpnValue {
  nominalBps: number;
  nonLuxuryDpp: Fraction;
  luxuryDpp: Fraction;
}
export interface SptDeadlineValue {
  month: number;
  day: number;
}
export interface SptGraceValue {
  penaltyWaivedUntil: string | null;
}

export interface TaxRuleValueMap {
  "pph.pasal17": readonly Pasal17Band[];
  "pph.ptkp": PtkpValue;
  "pph.biayaJabatan": BiayaJabatanValue;
  "pph.pkpRounding": PkpRoundingValue;
  "pph.zakat": ZakatValue;
  "pph.nppn": NppnValue;
  "pph21.terTables": TerTablesValue;
  "pph21.terCategoryMap": TerCategoryMapValue;
  "pph21.dtp": DtpValue;
  "final.depositoInterest": FinalRateValue;
  "final.savingsInterest": FinalRateValue;
  "final.dividend": DividendValue;
  "final.idxShareSale": IdxShareValue;
  "final.sbnCoupon": FinalRateValue;
  "final.mutualFund": FinalRateValue;
  "final.p2p": FinalRateValue;
  "final.cryptoDomestic": FinalRateValue;
  "final.cryptoForeign": FinalRateValue;
  "final.gold": FinalRateValue;
  "final.rental": FinalRateValue;
  "umkm.final": UmkmValue;
  "ppn.rate": PpnValue;
  "filing.sptDeadline": SptDeadlineValue;
  "filing.sptGrace": SptGraceValue;
}
export type TaxRuleKey = keyof TaxRuleValueMap;

export interface TaxRuleEntry<K extends TaxRuleKey = TaxRuleKey> {
  key: K;
  value: TaxRuleValueMap[K];
  /** Inclusive WIB calendar dates, "YYYY-MM-DD"; effectiveTo null = still in force. */
  effectiveFrom: string;
  effectiveTo: string | null;
  legalBasis: string;
  source: string | null;
  verified: boolean;
  note?: string;
}

export interface TaxRuleRef {
  key: TaxRuleKey;
  legalBasis: string;
  source: string | null;
  verified: boolean;
  effectiveFrom: string;
  effectiveTo: string | null;
  outOfRange: boolean;
  note: string | null;
}

export type AnyTaxRuleEntry = {
  [K in TaxRuleKey]: TaxRuleEntry<K>;
}[TaxRuleKey];
```

`apps/api/src/tax-rules/tax-rules.registry.spec.ts`:

```ts
import {
  TAX_RULES,
  listRuleRefs,
  monthStartDate,
  ruleAt,
  wibDateString,
  yearEndDate,
} from "./tax-rules.registry";

import type { TaxRuleKey } from "./tax-rule.types";

const nextDay = (d: string) => {
  const t = new Date(`${d}T00:00:00Z`);
  t.setUTCDate(t.getUTCDate() + 1);
  return t.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

describe("tax rule registry integrity", () => {
  const byKey = new Map<TaxRuleKey, (typeof TAX_RULES)[number][]>();
  for (const e of TAX_RULES) byKey.set(e.key, [...(byKey.get(e.key) ?? []), e]);

  it("every key's entries are contiguous, non-overlapping and end open-ended", () => {
    for (const [key, entries] of byKey) {
      const sorted = [...entries].sort((a, b) =>
        a.effectiveFrom.localeCompare(b.effectiveFrom),
      );
      sorted.forEach((e, i) => {
        const next = sorted[i + 1];
        if (next)
          expect({ key, gapFree: nextDay(e.effectiveTo ?? "") }).toEqual({
            key,
            gapFree: next.effectiveFrom,
          });
        else expect({ key, open: e.effectiveTo }).toEqual({ key, open: null });
      });
    }
  });

  it("every entry names a legal basis", () => {
    for (const e of TAX_RULES) expect(e.legalBasis.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("TER tables ascend strictly and end with an open band; A/B/C have 44/40/41 bands", () => {
    const { value } = ruleAt("pph21.terTables", "2026-01-01");
    for (const cat of ["A", "B", "C"] as const) {
      const t = value[cat];
      t.slice(0, -1).forEach((b, i) => {
        expect(b.upTo).not.toBeNull();
        if (i > 0) expect(b.upTo! > t[i - 1]!.upTo!).toBe(true);
        expect(t[i + 1]!.rateBps).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(b.rateBps);
      });
      expect(t[t.length - 1]!.upTo).toBeNull();
    }
    expect([value.A.length, value.B.length, value.C.length]).toEqual([
      44, 40, 41,
    ]);
  });
});

describe("ruleAt effective-date switching", () => {
  it("crypto domestic 10 bps until 2025-07-31, 21 bps from 2025-08-01", () => {
    expect(ruleAt("final.cryptoDomestic", "2025-07-31").value.rateBps).toBe(10);
    expect(ruleAt("final.cryptoDomestic", "2025-08-01").value.rateBps).toBe(21);
    expect(ruleAt("final.cryptoForeign", "2025-08-01").value.rateBps).toBe(100);
  });

  it("PPN: 2024 DPP 1/1 @11%, Jan-2025 luxury 11/12, Feb-2025 luxury 12/12", () => {
    expect(ruleAt("ppn.rate", "2024-12-31").value.nominalBps).toBe(1100);
    expect(ruleAt("ppn.rate", "2025-01-15").value.luxuryDpp).toEqual({
      num: 11n,
      den: 12n,
    });
    expect(ruleAt("ppn.rate", "2025-02-01").value.luxuryDpp).toEqual({
      num: 12n,
      den: 12n,
    });
  });

  it("DTP: 2024 none, pariwisata only from 2025-10-01, 2026 requires validated NIK", () => {
    expect(ruleAt("pph21.dtp", "2024-06-01").value.sectors).toEqual([]);
    expect(ruleAt("pph21.dtp", "2025-09-01").value.sectors).not.toContain(
      "pariwisata",
    );
    expect(ruleAt("pph21.dtp", "2025-10-01").value.sectors).toContain(
      "pariwisata",
    );
    expect(ruleAt("pph21.dtp", "2026-03-01").value.requiresValidatedNik).toBe(
      true,
    );
    expect(ruleAt("pph21.dtp", "2027-01-01").value.sectors).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("UMKM time limit 7 until 2026-04-21, none from 2026-04-22", () => {
    expect(
      ruleAt("umkm.final", "2026-04-21").value.individualTimeLimitYears,
    ).toBe(7);
    expect(
      ruleAt("umkm.final", "2026-04-22").value.individualTimeLimitYears,
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it("a date before the earliest entry resolves to it with outOfRange", () => {
    const r = ruleAt("pph.pasal17", yearEndDate(2019));
    expect(r.ref.outOfRange).toBe(true);
    expect(r.value[0]!.upTo).toBe(60_000_000n);
    expect(ruleAt("pph.pasal17", yearEndDate(2022)).ref.outOfRange).toBe(false);
  });

  it("SPT grace 2026-04-30 only for the 2026 filing season", () => {
    expect(
      ruleAt("filing.sptGrace", "2026-03-31").value.penaltyWaivedUntil,
    ).toBe("2026-04-30");
    expect(
      ruleAt("filing.sptGrace", "2025-03-31").value.penaltyWaivedUntil,
    ).toBeNull();
    expect(
      ruleAt("filing.sptGrace", "2027-03-31").value.penaltyWaivedUntil,
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rejects malformed dates", () => {
    expect(() => ruleAt("ppn.rate", "2025-1-1")).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
});

describe("date helpers and refs", () => {
  it("wibDateString uses the Asia/Jakarta calendar day", () => {
    expect(wibDateString(new Date("2025-07-31T17:00:00Z"))).toBe("2025-08-01");
    expect(wibDateString(new Date("2025-07-31T16:59:59Z"))).toBe("2025-07-31");
  });
  it("monthStartDate pads", () =>
    expect(monthStartDate(2026, 4)).toBe("2026-04-01"));
  it("listRuleRefs returns one ref per requested key", () => {
    const refs = listRuleRefs("2026-09-26", [
      "ppn.rate",
      "final.cryptoDomestic",
    ]);
    expect(refs.map((r) => r.key)).toEqual([
      "ppn.rate",
      "final.cryptoDomestic",
    ]);
    expect(refs[1]).toMatchObject({
      verified: true,
      source: "https://www.pajak.go.id/en/node/117234",
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement the registry**

`apps/api/src/tax-rules/tax-rules.registry.ts`:

```ts
import { FINAL_RULES } from "./data/final.rules";
import { PPH21_RULES } from "./data/pph21.rules";
import { PPH_RULES } from "./data/pph.rules";

import type {
  AnyTaxRuleEntry,
  TaxRuleEntry,
  TaxRuleKey,
  TaxRuleRef,
  TaxRuleValueMap,
} from "./tax-rule.types";

export const TAX_RULES: readonly AnyTaxRuleEntry[] = [
  ...PPH_RULES,
  ...PPH21_RULES,
  ...FINAL_RULES,
];

const DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const WIB_OFFSET_MS = 7 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export interface ResolvedRule<K extends TaxRuleKey> {
  value: TaxRuleValueMap[K];
  ref: TaxRuleRef;
}

function toRef(e: AnyTaxRuleEntry, outOfRange: boolean): TaxRuleRef {
  return {
    key: e.key,
    legalBasis: e.legalBasis,
    source: e.source,
    verified: e.verified,
    effectiveFrom: e.effectiveFrom,
    effectiveTo: e.effectiveTo,
    outOfRange,
    note: e.note ?? null,
  };
}

export function ruleAt<K extends TaxRuleKey>(
  key: K,
  date: string,
): ResolvedRule<K> {
  if (!DATE.test(date)) throw new RangeError(`bad rule date: ${date}`);
  const entries = TAX_RULES.filter((e) => e.key === key).sort((a, b) =>
    a.effectiveFrom.localeCompare(b.effectiveFrom),
  ) as unknown as TaxRuleEntry<K>[];
  const first = entries[0];
  if (!first) throw new Error(`no tax rule for ${key}`);
  const hit = entries.find(
    (e) =>
      e.effectiveFrom <= date &&
      (e.effectiveTo === null || date <= e.effectiveTo),
  );
  if (hit)
    return { value: hit.value, ref: toRef(hit as AnyTaxRuleEntry, false) };
  if (date < first.effectiveFrom)
    return { value: first.value, ref: toRef(first as AnyTaxRuleEntry, true) };
  throw new Error(`tax rule ${key} has no entry covering ${date}`);
}

export function listRuleRefs(
  date: string,
  keys?: readonly TaxRuleKey[],
): TaxRuleRef[] {
  const all = keys ?? [...new Set(TAX_RULES.map((e) => e.key))];
  return all.map((k) => ruleAt(k, date).ref);
}

export function uniqueRefs(refs: readonly TaxRuleRef[]): TaxRuleRef[] {
  const seen = new Map<string, TaxRuleRef>();
  for (const r of refs) seen.set(`${r.key}|${r.effectiveFrom}`, r);
  return [...seen.values()];
}

export const yearEndDate = (year: number) => `${year}-12-31`;
export const monthStartDate = (year: number, month: number) =>
  `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}-01`;
export const wibDateString = (d: Date) =>
  new Date(d.getTime() + WIB_OFFSET_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
```

**Interfaces:**

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `ptkp.ts` and `pph-op.engine.ts`**

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/ptkp.ts`:

```ts
import { ruleAt } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import type { TaxRuleRef } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";

export type MaritalStatus = "TK" | "K" | "KI";
export interface TaxStatus {
  maritalStatus: MaritalStatus;
  dependents: number;
}

export function ptkpFor(
  status: TaxStatus,
  date: string,
): { ptkp: bigint; ref: TaxRuleRef } {
  const { value, ref } = ruleAt("pph.ptkp", date);
  const d = status.dependents;
  if (!Number.isInteger(d) || d < 0 || d > value.maxDependents) {
    throw new RangeError(`dependents must be 0..${value.maxDependents}`);
  }
  const married = status.maritalStatus === "TK" ? 0n : value.marriedAddOn;
  const wife =
    status.maritalStatus === "KI" ? value.combinedWifeIncomeAddOn : 0n;
  return {
    ptkp: value.base + married + wife + BigInt(d) * value.dependentAddOn,
    ref,
  };
}
```

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/pph-op.engine.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ruleAt,
  uniqueRefs,
  yearEndDate,
} from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import { divHalfUp, floorTo } from "./money";
import { ptkpFor } from "./ptkp";

import type { TaxRuleRef } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";
import type { TaxStatus } from "./ptkp";

export interface PphOpInput {
  taxYear: number;
  status: TaxStatus | null;
  ptkpOverride?: bigint;
  employmentGross: bigint;
  pensionContribution: bigint;
  freelance: { gross: bigint; nppnBps: number } | null;
  otherNetIncome: bigint;
  zakat: bigint;
  withheldPph21: bigint;
}

export interface PphOpBracket {
  from: bigint;
  upTo: bigint | null;
  rateBps: number;
  label: string;
  taxable: bigint;
  tax: bigint;
}

export interface PphOpResult {
  taxYear: number;
  ptkp: bigint;
  biayaJabatanHundredths: bigint;
  biayaJabatan: bigint;
  pensionDeduction: bigint;
  freelanceNet: bigint;
  neto: bigint;
  zakatApplied: bigint;
  pkp: bigint;
  brackets: PphOpBracket[];
  tax: bigint;
  withheld: bigint;
  balance: bigint;
  rules: TaxRuleRef[];
}

const min = (a: bigint, b: bigint) => (a < b ? a : b);
const max = (a: bigint, b: bigint) => (a > b ? a : b);

export function pasal17Tax(pkp: bigint, date: string) {
  const { value, ref } = ruleAt("pph.pasal17", date);
  const brackets: PphOpBracket[] = [];
  let prev = 0n;
  for (const band of value) {
    if (pkp <= prev) break;
    const top = band.upTo === null || pkp < band.upTo ? pkp : band.upTo;
    const taxable = top - prev;
    brackets.push({
      from: prev,
      upTo: band.upTo,
      rateBps: band.rateBps,
      label: band.label,
      taxable,
      tax: divHalfUp(taxable * BigInt(band.rateBps), 10_000n),
    });
    if (band.upTo === null) break;
    prev = band.upTo;
  }
  return { brackets, tax: brackets.reduce((s, b) => s + b.tax, 0n), ref };
}

export function computePphOp(input: PphOpInput): PphOpResult {
  const date = yearEndDate(input.taxYear);
  const refs: TaxRuleRef[] = [];

  let ptkp: bigint;
  if (input.status) {
    const p = ptkpFor(input.status, date);
    ptkp = p.ptkp;
    refs.push(p.ref);
  } else if (input.ptkpOverride !== undefined) {
    ptkp = input.ptkpOverride;
  } else {
    throw new RangeError("status or ptkpOverride is required");
  }

  const bj = ruleAt("pph.biayaJabatan", date);
  refs.push(bj.ref);
  const gross100 = input.employmentGross * 100n;
  const bj100 = min(
    (input.employmentGross * BigInt(bj.value.rateBps)) / 100n,
    bj.value.capAnnual * 100n,
  );
  const pension100 = min(
    input.pensionContribution * 100n,
    max(0n, gross100 - bj100),
  );
  const employmentNet100 = max(0n, gross100 - bj100 - pension100);

  let freelanceNet = 0n;
  if (input.freelance) {
    const { gross, nppnBps } = input.freelance;
    if (!Number.isInteger(nppnBps) || nppnBps < 1 || nppnBps > 10_000) {
      throw new RangeError("nppnBps must be 1..10000");
    }
    freelanceNet = divHalfUp(gross * BigInt(nppnBps), 10_000n);
    refs.push(ruleAt("pph.nppn", date).ref);
  }

  const neto100 =
    employmentNet100 + freelanceNet * 100n + input.otherNetIncome * 100n;
  const zakatRule = ruleAt("pph.zakat", date);
  refs.push(zakatRule.ref);
  const zakat100 = min(input.zakat * 100n, neto100);

  const rounding = ruleAt("pph.pkpRounding", date);
  refs.push(rounding.ref);
  const pkp = floorTo(
    (neto100 - zakat100 - ptkp * 100n) / 100n,
    rounding.value.unit,
  );

  const p17 = pasal17Tax(pkp, date);
  refs.push(p17.ref);

  return {
    taxYear: input.taxYear,
    ptkp,
    biayaJabatanHundredths: bj100,
    biayaJabatan: divHalfUp(bj100, 100n),
    pensionDeduction: divHalfUp(pension100, 100n),
    freelanceNet,
    neto: divHalfUp(neto100, 100n),
    zakatApplied: divHalfUp(zakat100, 100n),
    pkp,
    brackets: p17.brackets,
    tax: p17.tax,
    withheld: input.withheldPph21,
    balance: p17.tax - input.withheldPph21,
    rules: uniqueRefs(refs),
  };
}

export function computeLegacyNetTax(
  gross: bigint,
  ptkp: bigint,
  zakat: bigint,
  taxYear: number,
): bigint {
  return computePphOp({
    taxYear,
    status: null,
    ptkpOverride: ptkp,
    employmentGross: gross,
    pensionContribution: 0n,
    freelance: null,
    otherNetIncome: 0n,
    zakat,
    withheldPph21: 0n,
  }).tax;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Make `pph21.util.ts` read the registry (public float calculator keeps byte-identical output)**

Replace the whole file with:

```ts
import { ruleAt, wibDateString } from "../../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

// Float path kept only for the public POST /calculators/pph21 (IsNumber input). `bps / 10_000`
// yields the same doubles as the old 0.05/0.15/… literals, so responses stay byte-identical.
const today = () => wibDateString(new Date());

export interface Pph21BracketTax {
  bracket: string;
  taxableAmount: number;
  rate: number;
  tax: number;
}

export function pph21DefaultPtkp(date: string = today()): number {
  return Number(ruleAt("pph.ptkp", date).value.base);
}

export function roundPkpDown(pkpRaw: number, date: string = today()): number {
  const unit = Number(ruleAt("pph.pkpRounding", date).value.unit);
  return Math.floor(Math.max(0, pkpRaw) / unit) * unit;
}

export function computePkp(
  grossIncome: number,
  ptkp: number,
  zakatDeduction = 0,
  date: string = today(),
): { biayaJabatan: number; pkp: number } {
  const bj = ruleAt("pph.biayaJabatan", date).value;
  const biayaJabatan = Math.min(
    grossIncome * (bj.rateBps / 10_000),
    Number(bj.capAnnual),
  );
  const pkpRaw = grossIncome - biayaJabatan - ptkp - zakatDeduction;
  return { biayaJabatan, pkp: roundPkpDown(pkpRaw, date) };
}

export function computeTaxBrackets(
  pkp: number,
  date: string = today(),
): Pph21BracketTax[] {
  const breakdown: Pph21BracketTax[] = [];
  let remaining = pkp;
  let previousLimit = 0;
  for (const band of ruleAt("pph.pasal17", date).value) {
    if (remaining <= 0) break;
    const limit = band.upTo === null ? Infinity : Number(band.upTo);
    const rate = band.rateBps / 10_000;
    const taxableAmount =
      limit === Infinity
        ? remaining
        : Math.min(remaining, limit - previousLimit);
    breakdown.push({
      bracket: band.label,
      taxableAmount,
      rate,
      tax: taxableAmount * rate,
    });
    remaining -= taxableAmount;
    previousLimit = limit;
  }
  return breakdown;
}

export function sumTaxRounded(breakdown: readonly Pph21BracketTax[]): number {
  return Math.round(breakdown.reduce((sum, b) => sum + b.tax, 0));
}
```

In `apps/api/src/calculators/calculators.service.ts`: import `pph21DefaultPtkp` instead of `PPH21_DEFAULT_PTKP` and use `const ptkp = dto.ptkp ?? pph21DefaultPtkp();`. Leave `PERPULUHAN_RATE`, `NISAB_GRAMS` and `INDICATIVE_GOLD_PRICE_PER_GRAM` in place (Key decision 3).

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const ROOT = join(__dirname, "..");
const GUARDED = [
  "common/utils/pph21.util.ts",
  "tax/tax.service.ts",
  ...readdirSync(join(ROOT, "tax-engine"))
    .filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts") && !f.endsWith(".spec.ts"))
    .map((f) => `tax-engine/${f}`),
  ...readdirSync(join(ROOT, "tax"))
    .filter((f) => f.endsWith(".service.ts"))
    .map((f) => `tax/${f}`),
];
// Rates/thresholds that must only live in tax-rules/data.
const FORBIDDEN = [
  /\b0\.0?5\b/,
  /\b0\.15\b/,
  /\b0\.25\b/,
  /\b0\.3\b/,
  /\b0\.35\b/,
  /\b0\.005\b/,
  /\b0\.0021\b/,
  /\b54_?000_?000n?\b/,
  /\b60_?000_?000n?\b/,
  /\b250_?000_?000n?\b/,
  /\b6_?000_?000n?\b/,
  /\b500_?000_?000n?\b/,
  /\b4_?800_?000_?000n?\b/,
  /\b7_?500_?000n?\b/,
];

describe("tax numbers live only in the rule registry", () => {
  it.each(GUARDED)("%s has no hardcoded tax rate/threshold", (rel) => {
    const src = readFileSync(join(ROOT, rel), "utf8");
    for (const re of FORBIDDEN)
      expect({ rel, match: src.match(re)?.[0] ?? null }).toEqual({
        rel,
        match: null,
      });
  });
});
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

```ts
export interface TerYearInput {
  taxYear: number;
  status: TaxStatus;
  monthlyGross: readonly bigint[]; // length 12, Jan..Dec
  monthlyPension: readonly bigint[]; // length 12
  zakat: bigint;
  dtp: DtpProfile | null;
}
export interface TerMonth {
  month: number;
  gross: bigint;
  category: TerCategory;
  rateBps: number | null; // null for December (true-up month)
  withholding: bigint; // December: the true-up, may be negative
  dtp: { eligible: boolean; reasons: DtpReason[]; amount: bigint } | null;
}
export interface TerYearResult {
  category: TerCategory;
  months: TerMonth[];
  annualTax: bigint;
  withheldJanNov: bigint;
  december: bigint;
  isRefund: boolean;
  dtpTotal: bigint;
  rules: TaxRuleRef[];
}
```

```ts
    expect(r.dtpTotal).toBe(

  });
});
```

```ts
import type { DtpProfile } from "./dtp.engine";

const permanent = (o: Partial<DtpProfile> = {}): DtpProfile => ({

  it("2026 requires a Coretax-validated NIK; 2025 does not", () => {

    expect(

        month: 9,

      month: 10,

  it("non-permanent: daily wage ≤ 500rb eligible; 500.001 not, unless 2026 monthly ≤ 10jt", () => {

        taxYear: 2026,
        month: 5,
        monthlyGross: 9_000_000n,
        profile: np(500_001n),
      }).eligible,
    ).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `dtp.engine.ts`**

```ts
import { monthStartDate, ruleAt } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import type { DtpSector, TaxRuleRef } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";

export type DtpEmployment = "permanent" | "non_permanent";
export type DtpReason =
  | "no_program"
  | "sector_not_covered"
  | "gross_above_cap"
  | "daily_wage_above_cap"
  | "nik_not_validated";
export interface DtpProfile {
  sector: DtpSector | null;
  employment: DtpEmployment;
  dailyWage: bigint | null;
  nikValidated: boolean;
}

export function dtpEligibility(args: {
  taxYear: number;
  month: number;
  monthlyGross: bigint;
  profile: DtpProfile;
}): { eligible: boolean; reasons: DtpReason[]; ref: TaxRuleRef } {
  const { value, ref } = ruleAt(
    "pph21.dtp",
    monthStartDate(args.taxYear, args.month),
  );
  if (value.sectors.length === 0)
    return { eligible: false, reasons: ["no_program"], ref };
  const reasons: DtpReason[] = [];
  const { profile } = args;
  if (profile.sector === null || !value.sectors.includes(profile.sector))
    reasons.push("sector_not_covered");
  if (profile.employment === "permanent") {
    if (args.monthlyGross > value.permanentMaxMonthlyGross)
      reasons.push("gross_above_cap");
  } else {
    const dailyOk =
      profile.dailyWage !== null &&
      profile.dailyWage <= value.nonPermanentMaxDailyWage;
    const monthlyOk =
      value.nonPermanentMaxMonthlyGross !== null &&
      args.monthlyGross <= value.nonPermanentMaxMonthlyGross;
    if (!dailyOk && !monthlyOk) reasons.push("daily_wage_above_cap");
  }
  if (value.requiresValidatedNik && !profile.nikValidated)
    reasons.push("nik_not_validated");
  return { eligible: reasons.length === 0, reasons, ref };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `ter.engine.ts`**

```ts
import {
  monthStartDate,
  ruleAt,
  uniqueRefs,
} from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import { dtpEligibility } from "./dtp.engine";
import { applyBps } from "./money";
import { computePphOp } from "./pph-op.engine";

import type { TaxRuleRef, TerCategory } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";
import type { DtpProfile, DtpReason } from "./dtp.engine";
import type { TaxStatus } from "./ptkp";

export class TerNotApplicableError extends Error {
  constructor(taxYear: number) {
    super(`TER berlaku mulai 2024 (tahun pajak ${taxYear})`);
  }
}

// … TerYearInput / TerMonth / TerYearResult exactly as in the Interfaces block …

export function terCategoryFor(status: TaxStatus, date: string) {
  const { value, ref } = ruleAt("pph21.terCategoryMap", date);
  const row = value[status.maritalStatus === "TK" ? "TK" : "K"];
  const category = row[status.dependents];
  if (category === undefined) throw new RangeError("dependents must be 0..3");
  return { category, ref };
}

export function terMonthly(args: {
  taxYear: number;
  month: number;
  status: TaxStatus;
  monthlyGross: bigint;
}) {
  const date = monthStartDate(args.taxYear, args.month);
  const tables = ruleAt("pph21.terTables", date);
  if (tables.ref.outOfRange) throw new TerNotApplicableError(args.taxYear);
  const cat = terCategoryFor(args.status, date);
  const band = tables.value[cat.category].find(
    (b) => b.upTo === null || args.monthlyGross <= b.upTo,
  )!;
  return {
    category: cat.category,
    rateBps: band.rateBps,
    withholding: applyBps(args.monthlyGross, band.rateBps),
    rules: [tables.ref, cat.ref],
  };
}

export function terYear(input: TerYearInput): TerYearResult {
  if (input.monthlyGross.length !== 12 || input.monthlyPension.length !== 12) {
    throw new RangeError("monthlyGross and monthlyPension need 12 entries");
  }
  const refs: TaxRuleRef[] = [];
  const months: TerMonth[] = [];
  let withheldJanNov = 0n;
  let dtpTotal = 0n;
  let category: TerCategory = "A";

  const dtpFor = (month: number, gross: bigint, amount: bigint) => {
    if (!input.dtp) return null;
    const d = dtpEligibility({
      taxYear: input.taxYear,
      month,
      monthlyGross: gross,
      profile: input.dtp,
    });
    refs.push(d.ref);
    const dtpAmount = d.eligible && amount > 0n ? amount : 0n;
    dtpTotal += dtpAmount;
    return {
      eligible: d.eligible,
      reasons: d.reasons as DtpReason[],
      amount: dtpAmount,
    };
  };

  for (let month = 1; month <= 11; month++) {
    const gross = input.monthlyGross[month - 1]!;
    const m = terMonthly({
      taxYear: input.taxYear,
      month,
      status: input.status,
      monthlyGross: gross,
    });
    refs.push(...m.rules);
    category = m.category;
    withheldJanNov += m.withholding;
    months.push({
      month,
      gross,
      category: m.category,
      rateBps: m.rateBps,
      withholding: m.withholding,
      dtp: dtpFor(month, gross, m.withholding),
    });
  }

  const annual = computePphOp({
    taxYear: input.taxYear,
    status: input.status,
    employmentGross: input.monthlyGross.reduce((s, g) => s + g, 0n),
    pensionContribution: input.monthlyPension.reduce((s, p) => s + p, 0n),
    freelance: null,
    otherNetIncome: 0n,
    zakat: input.zakat,
    withheldPph21: withheldJanNov,
  });
  refs.push(...annual.rules);
  const december = annual.balance;
  const decGross = input.monthlyGross[11]!;
  months.push({
    month: 12,
    gross: decGross,
    category,
    rateBps: null,
    withholding: december,
    dtp: dtpFor(12, decGross, december),
  });

  return {
    category,
    months,
    annualTax: annual.tax,
    withheldJanNov,
    december,
    isRefund: december < 0n,
    dtpTotal,
    rules: uniqueRefs(refs),
  };
}
```

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/api/src/tax-engine/final-tax.engine.ts` (+`.spec.ts`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax-engine/umkm.engine.ts` (+`.spec.ts`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax-engine/ppn.engine.ts` (+`.spec.ts`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax-engine/spt.engine.ts` (+`.spec.ts`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax-engine/dates.ts` (+`.spec.ts`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `ruleAt`, `monthStartDate`, `uniqueRefs`, `applyBps`, `divHalfUp`, `TaxRuleKey`, `TaxRuleRef`.
- Produces:
  - `dates.ts`: `daysBetween(from: string, to: string): number` (to − from, may be negative); `maxDate(a, b)`, `minDate(a, b)` on `"YYYY-MM-DD"` strings.
  - `type FinalInstrument = "bunga_deposito" | "bunga_tabungan" | "dividen" | "saham_idx" | "kupon_sbn" | "reksa_dana" | "p2p" | "kripto_domestik" | "kripto_luar" | "emas" | "sewa"` and `FINAL_INSTRUMENTS: readonly FinalInstrument[]` (same order)
  - `finalTaxFor(input: { instrument: FinalInstrument; grossAmount: bigint; date: string; reinvested?: boolean; principal?: bigint | null; rateBpsOverride?: number | null }): { rateBps: number | null; tax: bigint | null; rateSource: "rule" | "override" | "missing"; belowThreshold: boolean; ref: TaxRuleRef }`
  - `accruedInterest(args: { principal: bigint; rateHundredths: bigint; from: string; to: string | null; taxYear: number }): { interest: bigint; days: number }` — `rateHundredths` = % × 100 (4,25 % → `425n`); `to` exclusive, `null` = open.
  - `umkmYear(input: { taxYear: number; monthlyOmzet: readonly bigint[]; firstRegisteredYear: number | null }): UmkmResult` with `UmkmMonth { month; omzet; cumulative; taxable; tax: bigint | null; eligible: boolean }` and `UmkmResult { months; totalOmzet; totalTax; exceedsCeiling; ineligibleReason: "time_limit" | null; rules }`
  - `ppnPreview(input: { date: string; amount: bigint; luxury: boolean; priceIncludesPpn: boolean }): { priceExcludingPpn: bigint; dpp: bigint; ppn: bigint; totalWithPpn: bigint; nominalBps: number; effectiveBps: number; rules: TaxRuleRef[] }`
  - `sptTiming(taxYear: number, today: string): { deadline: string; graceUntil: string | null; daysToDeadline: number; pastDeadline: boolean; withinGrace: boolean; rules: TaxRuleRef[] }`

```ts
      accruedInterest({
        principal: 50n * J,
        rateHundredths: 640n,
        from: "2025-06-01",
        to: null,
        taxYear: 2026,
      }),
    ).toEqual({ interest: 3_200_000n, days: 365 });
  });
  it("matured before the tax year → 0", () => {
    expect(
      accruedInterest({
        principal: 50n * J,
        rateHundredths: 640n,
        from: "2024-01-01",
        to: "2025-06-01",
        taxYear: 2026,
      }),
    ).toEqual({ interest: 0n, days: 0 });
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/umkm.engine.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { umkmYear } from "./umkm.engine";

const J = 1_000_000n;
const twelve = (v: bigint) => Array.from({ length: 12 }, () => v);

describe("umkmYear", () => {
  it("12 × 70jt: exempt until cumulative 500jt, Aug pays on 60jt, total 1.700.000", () => {
    const r = umkmYear({
      taxYear: 2026,
      monthlyOmzet: twelve(70n * J),
      firstRegisteredYear: null,
    });
    expect(r.months.map((m) => m.tax)).toEqual([
      0n,
      0n,
      0n,
      0n,
      0n,
      0n,
      0n,
      300_000n,
      350_000n,
      350_000n,
      350_000n,
      350_000n,
    ]);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({
      totalOmzet: 840n * J,
      totalTax: 1_700_000n,
      exceedsCeiling: false,
      ineligibleReason: null,
    });
  });
  it("exactly Rp4,8M is within the ceiling; one rupiah more exceeds it", () => {
    expect(
      umkmYear({
        taxYear: 2026,
        monthlyOmzet: twelve(400n * J),
        firstRegisteredYear: null,
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ totalTax: 21_500_000n, exceedsCeiling: false });
    const over = [...twelve(400n * J).slice(0, 11), 400n * J + 1n];
    expect(
      umkmYear({ taxYear: 2026, monthlyOmzet: over, firstRegisteredYear: null })
        .exceedsCeiling,
    ).toBe(true);
  });
  it("half-up per month: excess 123.456.789 → 617.284", () => {
    const r = umkmYear({
      taxYear: 2026,
      monthlyOmzet: [500n * J, 123_456_789n, ...twelve(0n).slice(2)],
      firstRegisteredYear: null,
    });
    expect(r.months[1]!.tax).toBe(617_284n);
  });
  it("registered 2018: tax year 2025 fully ineligible (7-year limit)", () => {
    const r = umkmYear({
      taxYear: 2025,
      monthlyOmzet: twelve(70n * J),
      firstRegisteredYear: 2018,
    });
    expect(r.months.every((m) => !m.eligible && m.tax === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ ineligibleReason: "time_limit", totalTax: 0n });
  });
  it("registered 2018: tax year 2026 Jan–Apr ineligible, May–Dec eligible (PP 20/2026 from 22 Apr)", () => {
    const r = umkmYear({
      taxYear: 2026,
      monthlyOmzet: twelve(70n * J),
      firstRegisteredYear: 2018,
    });
    expect(r.months.map((m) => m.eligible)).toEqual([
      false,
      false,
      false,
      false,
      true,
      true,
      true,
      true,
      true,
      true,
      true,
      true,
    ]);
    expect(r.totalTax).toBe(1_700_000n);
  });
  it("needs 12 months", () => {
    expect(() =>
      umkmYear({
        taxYear: 2026,
        monthlyOmzet: [1n],
        firstRegisteredYear: null,
      }),
    ).toThrow(RangeError);
  });
});
```

(The cumulative exemption counts every month's omzet, eligible or not. May–Dec in the last test are the same months that carry tax in the first test, so the total is 1.700.000 again.)

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/dates.ts`:

```ts
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
export const daysBetween = (from: string, to: string) =>
  Math.round(
    (Date.parse(`${to}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${from}T00:00:00Z`)) / DAY_MS,
  );
export const maxDate = (a: string, b: string) => (a > b ? a : b);
export const minDate = (a: string, b: string) => (a < b ? a : b);
```

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/final-tax.engine.ts`:

```ts
import { ruleAt } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import { daysBetween, maxDate, minDate } from "./dates";
import { applyBps, divHalfUp } from "./money";

import type {
  FinalRateValue,
  TaxRuleKey,
  TaxRuleRef,
} from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";

export const FINAL_INSTRUMENTS = [
  "bunga_deposito",
  "bunga_tabungan",
  "dividen",
  "saham_idx",
  "kupon_sbn",
  "reksa_dana",
  "p2p",
  "kripto_domestik",
  "kripto_luar",
  "emas",
  "sewa",
] as const;
export type FinalInstrument = (typeof FINAL_INSTRUMENTS)[number];

const SIMPLE_KEY: Record<
  Exclude<FinalInstrument, "dividen" | "saham_idx">,
  TaxRuleKey
> = {
  bunga_deposito: "final.depositoInterest",
  bunga_tabungan: "final.savingsInterest",
  kupon_sbn: "final.sbnCoupon",
  reksa_dana: "final.mutualFund",
  p2p: "final.p2p",
  kripto_domestik: "final.cryptoDomestic",
  kripto_luar: "final.cryptoForeign",
  emas: "final.gold",
  sewa: "final.rental",
};

function ruleRate(
  instrument: FinalInstrument,
  date: string,
  reinvested: boolean,
) {
  if (instrument === "dividen") {
    const r = ruleAt("final.dividend", date);
    return {
      rateBps: reinvested ? r.value.reinvestedBps : r.value.notReinvestedBps,
      minPrincipal: null,
      ref: r.ref,
    };
  }
  if (instrument === "saham_idx") {
    const r = ruleAt("final.idxShareSale", date);
    return { rateBps: r.value.rateBps, minPrincipal: null, ref: r.ref };
  }
  const r = ruleAt(SIMPLE_KEY[instrument], date) as {
    value: FinalRateValue;
    ref: TaxRuleRef;
  };
  return {
    rateBps: r.value.rateBps,
    minPrincipal: r.value.minPrincipal,
    ref: r.ref,
  };
}

export function finalTaxFor(input: {
  instrument: FinalInstrument;
  grossAmount: bigint;
  date: string;
  reinvested?: boolean;
  principal?: bigint | null;
  rateBpsOverride?: number | null;
}) {
  const rule = ruleRate(
    input.instrument,
    input.date,
    input.reinvested === true,
  );
  const override = input.rateBpsOverride ?? null;
  if (
    override !== null &&
    (!Number.isInteger(override) || override < 0 || override > 10_000)
  ) {
    throw new RangeError("rateBpsOverride must be 0..10000");
  }
  const belowThreshold =
    rule.minPrincipal !== null &&
    input.principal != null &&
    input.principal <= rule.minPrincipal;
  const rateBps = override ?? rule.rateBps;
  const rateSource =
    override !== null ? "override" : rule.rateBps === null ? "missing" : "rule";
  const tax =
    rateBps === null
      ? null
      : belowThreshold
        ? 0n
        : applyBps(input.grossAmount, rateBps);
  return { rateBps, tax, rateSource, belowThreshold, ref: rule.ref } as const;
}

export function accruedInterest(args: {
  principal: bigint;
  rateHundredths: bigint;
  from: string;
  to: string | null;
  taxYear: number;
}) {
  const start = maxDate(args.from, `${args.taxYear}-01-01`);
  const end = minDate(
    args.to ?? `${args.taxYear + 1}-01-01`,
    `${args.taxYear + 1}-01-01`,
  );
  const days = Math.max(0, daysBetween(start, end));
  if (days === 0) return { interest: 0n, days: 0 };
  return {
    interest: divHalfUp(
      args.principal * args.rateHundredths * BigInt(days),
      10_000n * 365n,
    ),
    days,
  };
}
```

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/umkm.engine.ts`:

```ts
import {
  monthStartDate,
  ruleAt,
  uniqueRefs,
} from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import { applyBps } from "./money";

import type { TaxRuleRef } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";

export interface UmkmMonth {
  month: number;
  omzet: bigint;
  cumulative: bigint;
  taxable: bigint;
  tax: bigint | null;
  eligible: boolean;
}
export interface UmkmResult {
  months: UmkmMonth[];
  totalOmzet: bigint;
  totalTax: bigint;
  exceedsCeiling: boolean;
  ineligibleReason: "time_limit" | null;
  rules: TaxRuleRef[];
}

export function umkmYear(input: {
  taxYear: number;
  monthlyOmzet: readonly bigint[];
  firstRegisteredYear: number | null;
}): UmkmResult {
  if (input.monthlyOmzet.length !== 12)
    throw new RangeError("monthlyOmzet needs 12 entries");
  const refs: TaxRuleRef[] = [];
  const months: UmkmMonth[] = [];
  let cumulative = 0n;
  let totalTax = 0n;
  let exceedsCeiling = false;
  let ineligibleReason: "time_limit" | null = null;

  input.monthlyOmzet.forEach((omzet, i) => {
    const month = i + 1;
    const { value, ref } = ruleAt(
      "umkm.final",
      monthStartDate(input.taxYear, month),
    );
    refs.push(ref);
    const before = cumulative;
    cumulative += omzet;
    if (cumulative > value.turnoverCeiling) exceedsCeiling = true;
    const limit = value.individualTimeLimitYears;
    const eligible = !(
      limit !== null &&
      input.firstRegisteredYear !== null &&
      input.taxYear >= input.firstRegisteredYear + limit
    );
    if (!eligible) {
      ineligibleReason = "time_limit";
      months.push({
        month,
        omzet,
        cumulative,
        taxable: 0n,
        tax: null,
        eligible,
      });
      return;
    }
    const ex = value.individualExemption;
    const taxable =
      cumulative <= ex ? 0n : before >= ex ? omzet : cumulative - ex;
    const tax = applyBps(taxable, value.rateBps);
    totalTax += tax;
    months.push({ month, omzet, cumulative, taxable, tax, eligible });
  });

  return {
    months,
    totalOmzet: cumulative,
    totalTax,
    exceedsCeiling,
    ineligibleReason,
    rules: uniqueRefs(refs),
  };
}
```

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/ppn.engine.ts`:

```ts
import { ruleAt } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import { divHalfUp } from "./money";

export function ppnPreview(input: {
  date: string;
  amount: bigint;
  luxury: boolean;
  priceIncludesPpn: boolean;
}) {
  const { value, ref } = ruleAt("ppn.rate", input.date);
  const { num, den } = input.luxury ? value.luxuryDpp : value.nonLuxuryDpp;
  const nominal = BigInt(value.nominalBps);
  const scale = den * 10_000n;
  const base = input.priceIncludesPpn
    ? divHalfUp(input.amount * scale, scale + num * nominal)
    : input.amount;
  const ppn = input.priceIncludesPpn
    ? input.amount - base
    : divHalfUp(base * num * nominal, scale);
  return {
    priceExcludingPpn: base,
    dpp: divHalfUp(base * num, den),
    ppn,
    totalWithPpn: base + ppn,
    nominalBps: value.nominalBps,
    effectiveBps: Number(divHalfUp(nominal * num, den)),
    rules: [ref],
  };
}
```

`apps/api/src/tax-engine/spt.engine.ts`:

```ts
import { ruleAt } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";

import { daysBetween } from "./dates";

const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

export function sptTiming(taxYear: number, today: string) {
  const filingYear = taxYear + 1;
  const d = ruleAt("filing.sptDeadline", `${filingYear}-01-01`);
  const deadline = `${filingYear}-${pad(d.value.month)}-${pad(d.value.day)}`;
  const g = ruleAt("filing.sptGrace", deadline);
  const graceUntil = g.value.penaltyWaivedUntil;
  const daysToDeadline = daysBetween(today, deadline);
  const pastDeadline = today > deadline;
  return {
    deadline,
    graceUntil,
    daysToDeadline,
    pastDeadline,
    withinGrace: pastDeadline && graceUntil !== null && today <= graceUntil,
    rules: [d.ref, g.ref],
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the migration**

`apps/api/prisma/migrations/20260926180000_revisi_l_tax/migration.sql`:

```sql
-- Revisi L: detailed PPh OP on tax_records, manual final-income lines, UMKM omzet per year, crypto exchange kind.
CREATE TYPE "TaxMaritalStatus" AS ENUM ('TK', 'K', 'KI');
CREATE TYPE "TaxCalcMode" AS ENUM ('legacy', 'detailed');
CREATE TYPE "TaxFinalInstrument" AS ENUM ('bunga_deposito', 'bunga_tabungan', 'dividen', 'saham_idx', 'kupon_sbn', 'reksa_dana', 'p2p', 'kripto_domestik', 'kripto_luar', 'emas', 'sewa');
CREATE TYPE "CryptoExchangeKind" AS ENUM ('domestik', 'luar_negeri');

ALTER TABLE "tax_records"
  ADD COLUMN "calcMode" "TaxCalcMode" NOT NULL DEFAULT 'legacy',
  ADD COLUMN "maritalStatus" "TaxMaritalStatus",
  ADD COLUMN "dependents" INTEGER,
  ADD COLUMN "pensionContribution" BIGINT,
  ADD COLUMN "freelanceGross" BIGINT,
  ADD COLUMN "nppnRateBps" INTEGER,
  ADD COLUMN "otherNetIncome" BIGINT,
  ADD COLUMN "withheldPph21" BIGINT,
  ADD COLUMN "sptFiledAt" TIMESTAMP(3);

ALTER TABLE "investments" ADD COLUMN "cryptoExchange" "CryptoExchangeKind";

CREATE TABLE "tax_final_income_entries" (
    "id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "familyGroupId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "taxYear" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "instrument" "TaxFinalInstrument" NOT NULL,
    "grossAmount" BIGINT NOT NULL,
    "occurredAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL,
    "reinvested" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    "rateBpsOverride" INTEGER,
    "investmentId" TEXT,
    "accountId" TEXT,
    "notes" TEXT,
    "createdAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    "updatedAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "tax_final_income_entries_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("id")
);
CREATE INDEX "tax_final_income_entries_familyGroupId_taxYear_idx" ON "tax_final_income_entries"("familyGroupId", "taxYear");
CREATE INDEX "tax_final_income_entries_investmentId_idx" ON "tax_final_income_entries"("investmentId");
CREATE INDEX "tax_final_income_entries_accountId_idx" ON "tax_final_income_entries"("accountId");
ALTER TABLE "tax_final_income_entries" ADD CONSTRAINT "tax_final_income_entries_familyGroupId_fkey"
  FOREIGN KEY ("familyGroupId") REFERENCES "family_groups"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE "tax_final_income_entries" ADD CONSTRAINT "tax_final_income_entries_investmentId_fkey"
  FOREIGN KEY ("investmentId") REFERENCES "investments"("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE "tax_final_income_entries" ADD CONSTRAINT "tax_final_income_entries_accountId_fkey"
  FOREIGN KEY ("accountId") REFERENCES "accounts"("id") ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE;

CREATE TABLE "tax_umkm_years" (
    "id" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "familyGroupId" TEXT NOT NULL,
    "taxYear" INTEGER NOT NULL,
    "omzetByMonth" BIGINT[],
    "firstRegisteredYear" INTEGER,
    "createdAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    "updatedAt" TIMESTAMP(3) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT "tax_umkm_years_pkey" PRIMARY KEY ("id")
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "tax_umkm_years_familyGroupId_taxYear_key" ON "tax_umkm_years"("familyGroupId", "taxYear");
ALTER TABLE "tax_umkm_years" ADD CONSTRAINT "tax_umkm_years_familyGroupId_fkey"
  FOREIGN KEY ("familyGroupId") REFERENCES "family_groups"("id") ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE;
```

- Modify: `apps/api/src/calculators/calculators.controller.ts` (two routes), `apps/api/src/tax/tax.module.ts` (controllers order)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `computePphOp`, `terYear`, `TerNotApplicableError`, `umkmYear`, `ppnPreview`, `listRuleRefs`, `TAX_RULES`, `wibDateString`, `parseDigits`; contract types from Task 5.
- Produces:
  - Mappers: `toRuleRefDtos(refs: TaxRuleRef[]): TaxRuleRefDto[]`, `toPphOpResultDto(r: PphOpResult): PphOpResultDto`, `toTerResultDto(r: TerYearResult): TerPreviewResultDto`, `toUmkmResultDto(r: UmkmResult, taxYear: number, firstRegisteredYear: number | null, saved: boolean): UmkmResultDto`, `toPpnResultDto(r): PpnPreviewResultDto`, `mapEngineError(e: unknown): never` (RangeError → 400, TerNotApplicableError → 422, other → rethrow).
  - DTO → engine input: `pphOpInputFromDto(dto: PphOpPreviewDto): PphOpInput`.
  - Routes, all `@PageAccess("tax")` + `@CheckAbility(read TaxRecord)`, `@HttpCode(200)`:
    - `POST /family-groups/:familyGroupId/tax/pph-op/preview` → `PphOpResultDto`
    - `POST /family-groups/:familyGroupId/tax/ter/preview` → `TerPreviewResultDto`
    - `POST /family-groups/:familyGroupId/tax/umkm/preview` → `UmkmResultDto` (`saved: false`)
  - Public (under the existing `@Public()` calculators controller):
    - `GET /calculators/tax-rules?date=YYYY-MM-DD&keys=k1,k2` → `TaxRuleDetailDto[]` (ref + value, BigInt as digit strings; `date` defaults to today WIB; unknown key → 400)
    - `POST /calculators/ppn` → `PpnPreviewResultDto`
  - Harness: `buildTaxTestApp(opts: { controllers: Type[]; providers: Provider[] }): Promise<{ app: INestApplication; setRole(role: Role | null): void }>`

- [ ] **Step 1: Extract the shared HTTP harness**

`apps/api/src/tax/test-utils/tax-http-harness.ts` (lifted from `tax.controller.spec.ts`; that spec stays as is):

```ts
import "reflect-metadata";

import { ValidationPipe } from "@nestjs/common";
import { APP_GUARD } from "@nestjs/core";
import { Test } from "@nestjs/testing";

import { CaslAbilityFactory } from "../../casl/casl-ability.factory";
import { AbilitiesGuard } from "../../common/guards/abilities.guard";

import type { INestApplication, Provider, Type } from "@nestjs/common";
import type { Role } from "@prisma/client";
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from "express";

export const FAMILY_GROUP_ID = "fg-1";

export async function buildTaxTestApp(opts: {
  controllers: Type[];
  providers: Provider[];
}) {
  let role: Role | null = "owner";
  const moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({
    controllers: opts.controllers,
    providers: [
      ...opts.providers,
      CaslAbilityFactory,
      { provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: AbilitiesGuard },
    ],
  }).compile();
  const app: INestApplication = moduleRef.createNestApplication();
  app.use((req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    req.tenantContext = role
      ? {
          userId: `u-${role}`,
          familyGroupId: FAMILY_GROUP_ID,
          role,
          member: {
            id: `m-${role}`,
            userId: `u-${role}`,
            familyGroupId: FAMILY_GROUP_ID,
            role,
          },
        }
      : undefined;
    next();
  });
  app.useGlobalPipes(new ValidationPipe({ whitelist: true, transform: true }));
  await app.init();
  return {
    app,
    setRole: (r: Role | null) => {
      role = r;
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/api/src/tax/tax-preview.controller.spec.ts`:

```ts
import request from "supertest";

import { TaxPreviewController } from "./tax-preview.controller";
import {
  FAMILY_GROUP_ID,
  buildTaxTestApp,
} from "./test-utils/tax-http-harness";

import type { INestApplication } from "@nestjs/common";

const base = `/family-groups/${FAMILY_GROUP_ID}/tax`;
const twelve = (v: string) => Array.from({ length: 12 }, () => v);

describe("TaxPreviewController", () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let setRole: (
    r: "owner" | "admin_staff" | "dependant" | "operational" | null,
  ) => void;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, setRole } = await buildTaxTestApp({
      controllers: [TaxPreviewController],
      providers: [],
    }));
  });
  afterAll(() => app.close());
  beforeEach(() => setRole("owner"));

  const post = (path: string, body: unknown) =>
    request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`${base}/${path}`).send(body);

  it("PPh OP preview: K/1 120jt, pension 3,6jt, zakat 2,5jt → tax 2.245.000 with rules", async () => {
    const res = await post("pph-op/preview", {
      taxYear: 2026,
      maritalStatus: "K",
      dependents: 1,
      employmentGross: "120000000",
      pensionContribution: "3600000",
      zakat: "2500000",
      withheldPph21: "2000000",
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({
      ptkp: "63000000",
      pkp: "44900000",
      tax: "2245000",
      balance: "245000",
    });
    expect(res.body.brackets[0]).toMatchObject({
      rateBps: 500,
      label: "0 – 60 Juta",
      taxable: "44900000",
      tax: "2245000",
    });
    expect(
      res.body.rules.some(
        (r: { key: string; verified: boolean }) =>
          r.key === "pph.pasal17" && r.verified === false,
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it("PPh OP preview rejects dependents=4 and a float gross", async () => {
    expect(
      (
        await post("pph-op/preview", {
          taxYear: 2026,
          maritalStatus: "K",
          dependents: 4,
          employmentGross: "1",
        })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
    expect(
      (
        await post("pph-op/preview", {
          taxYear: 2026,
          maritalStatus: "K",
          dependents: 0,
          employmentGross: "1.5",
        })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
  });

  it("PPh OP preview requires nppnRateBps when freelanceGross > 0", async () => {
    const res = await post("pph-op/preview", {
      taxYear: 2026,
      maritalStatus: "TK",
      dependents: 0,
      employmentGross: "0",
      freelanceGross: "600000000",
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  it("TER preview: refund case returns a negative December as a string", async () => {
    const res = await post("ter/preview", {
      taxYear: 2026,
      maritalStatus: "TK",
      dependents: 0,
      monthlyGross: [
        "50000000",
        ...Array.from({ length: 11 }, () => "10000000"),
      ],
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({
      december: "-2000000",
      isRefund: true,
      withheldJanNov: "11000000",
    });
    expect(res.body.months).toHaveLength(12);
  });

  it("TER preview for 2023 is 422 (TER starts 2024)", async () => {
    expect(
      (
        await post("ter/preview", {
          taxYear: 2023,
          maritalStatus: "TK",
          dependents: 0,
          monthlyGross: twelve("1"),
        })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(422);
  });

  it("TER preview needs exactly 12 months", async () => {
    expect(
      (
        await post("ter/preview", {
          taxYear: 2026,
          maritalStatus: "TK",
          dependents: 0,
          monthlyGross: ["1"],
        })
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
  });

  it("TER preview with DTP profile flags eligibility per month", async () => {
    const res = await post("ter/preview", {
      taxYear: 2026,
      maritalStatus: "TK",
      dependents: 0,
      monthlyGross: twelve("10000000"),
      dtp: {
        sector: "tekstil_pakaian",
        employment: "permanent",
        nikValidated: true,
      },
    });
    expect(res.body.months[0].dtp).toEqual({
      eligible: true,
      reasons: [],
      amount: "200000",
    });
  });

  it("UMKM preview: 12 × 70jt → 1.700.000, saved=false", async () => {
    const res = await post("umkm/preview", {
      taxYear: 2026,
      monthlyOmzet: twelve("70000000"),
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toMatchObject({
      totalTax: "1700000",
      saved: false,
      exceedsCeiling: false,
    });
  });

  it("admin_staff may preview (read); dependant and operational may not", async () => {
    const body = {
      taxYear: 2026,
      maritalStatus: "TK",
      dependents: 0,
      employmentGross: "1",
    };
    setRole("admin_staff");
    expect((await post("pph-op/preview", body)).status).toBe(200);
    setRole("dependant");
    expect((await post("pph-op/preview", body)).status).toBe(403);
    setRole("operational");
    expect((await post("pph-op/preview", body)).status).toBe(403);
  });
});
```

`apps/api/src/calculators/calculators-tax-rules.spec.ts`:

```ts
import request from "supertest";

import { buildTaxTestApp } from "../tax/test-utils/tax-http-harness";

import { CalculatorsController } from "./calculators.controller";
import { CalculatorsService } from "./calculators.service";

import type { INestApplication } from "@nestjs/common";

describe("public tax-rule + PPN calculators", () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app } = await buildTaxTestApp({
      controllers: [CalculatorsController],
      providers: [CalculatorsService],
    }));
  });
  afterAll(() => app.close());

  it("GET /calculators/tax-rules returns refs for the asked keys at a date", async () => {
    const res = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(
      "/calculators/tax-rules?date=2025-07-31&keys=final.cryptoDomestic,ppn.rate",
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({
        key: "final.cryptoDomestic",
        effectiveFrom: "2022-05-01",
        verified: false,
      }),
      expect.objectContaining({
        key: "ppn.rate",
        effectiveFrom: "2025-02-01",
        verified: true,
      }),
    ]);
  });

  it("serves rule values with BigInt as digit strings", async () => {
    const res = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(
      "/calculators/tax-rules?date=2026-09-26&keys=final.savingsInterest",
    );
    expect(res.body[0]).toMatchObject({
      key: "final.savingsInterest",
      verified: false,
      value: { rateBps: 2000, minPrincipal: "7500000" },
    });
  });

  it("unknown key or malformed date → 400", async () => {
    expect(
      (
        await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(
          "/calculators/tax-rules?keys=nope",
        )
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
    expect(
      (
        await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(
          "/calculators/tax-rules?date=2025-7-1",
        )
      ).status,
    ).toBe(400);
  });

  it("POST /calculators/ppn: luxury on 2025-02-01 → 120.000; non-luxury → 110.000", async () => {
    const lux = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post("/calculators/ppn")
      .send({
        date: "2025-02-01",
        amount: "1000000",
        luxury: true,
        priceIncludesPpn: false,
      });
    expect(lux.status).toBe(200);
    expect(lux.body).toMatchObject({ ppn: "120000", effectiveBps: 1200 });
    const std = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post("/calculators/ppn")
      .send({
        date: "2026-09-26",
        amount: "1000000",
        luxury: false,
        priceIncludesPpn: false,
      });
    expect(std.body).toMatchObject({
      ppn: "110000",
      dpp: "916667",
      effectiveBps: 1100,
    });
  });
});
```
