```ts
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { Type } from "class-transformer";
import {
  ArrayMaxSize,
  ArrayMinSize,
  IsArray,
  IsBoolean,
  IsIn,
  IsInt,
  IsOptional,
  Matches,
  Max,
  Min,
  ValidateIf,
  ValidateNested,
} from "class-validator";

export const TAX_DIGITS = /^\d{1,15}$/;
const DIGITS_MSG = "must be a numeric string of up to 15 digits";
export const MARITAL = ["TK", "K", "KI"] as const;
export const DTP_SECTORS = [
  "alas_kaki",
  "tekstil_pakaian",
  "furnitur",
  "kulit",
  "pariwisata",
] as const;

export class PphOpPreviewDto {
  @ApiProperty() @IsInt() @Min(2000) @Max(2100) taxYear!: number;
  @ApiProperty({ enum: MARITAL })
  @IsIn(MARITAL)
  maritalStatus!: (typeof MARITAL)[number];
  @ApiProperty() @IsInt() @Min(0) @Max(3) dependents!: number;
  @ApiProperty()
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS, { message: `employmentGross ${DIGITS_MSG}` })
  employmentGross!: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS)
  pensionContribution?: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS)
  freelanceGross?: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ description: "NPPN % in basis points (5000 = 50%)" })
  @ValidateIf(
    (o: PphOpPreviewDto) =>
      o.freelanceGross !== undefined && !/^0+$/.test(o.freelanceGross),
  )
  @IsInt()
  @Min(1)
  @Max(10_000)
  nppnRateBps?: number;
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS)
  otherNetIncome?: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional() @IsOptional() @Matches(TAX_DIGITS) zakat?: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS)
  withheldPph21?: string;
}

export class TerDtpProfileDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ enum: DTP_SECTORS, nullable: true })
  @ValidateIf((o: TerDtpProfileDto) => o.sector !== null)
  @IsIn(DTP_SECTORS)
  sector!: (typeof DTP_SECTORS)[number] | null;
  @ApiProperty({ enum: ["permanent", "non_permanent"] })
  @IsIn(["permanent", "non_permanent"])
  employment!: "permanent" | "non_permanent";
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateIf((o: TerDtpProfileDto) => o.dailyWage !== null)
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS)
  dailyWage?: string | null;
  @ApiProperty() @IsBoolean() nikValidated!: boolean;
}

export class TerPreviewDto {
  @ApiProperty() @IsInt() @Min(2000) @Max(2100) taxYear!: number;
  @ApiProperty({ enum: MARITAL })
  @IsIn(MARITAL)
  maritalStatus!: (typeof MARITAL)[number];
  @ApiProperty() @IsInt() @Min(0) @Max(3) dependents!: number;
  @ApiProperty({ type: [String] })
  @IsArray()
  @ArrayMinSize(12)
  @ArrayMaxSize(12)
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS, { each: true })
  monthlyGross!: string[];
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: [String] })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsArray()
  @ArrayMinSize(12)
  @ArrayMaxSize(12)
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS, { each: true })
  monthlyPension?: string[];
  @ApiPropertyOptional() @IsOptional() @Matches(TAX_DIGITS) zakat?: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: TerDtpProfileDto, nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => TerDtpProfileDto)
  dtp?: TerDtpProfileDto | null;
}

export class UmkmPreviewDto {
  @ApiProperty() @IsInt() @Min(2000) @Max(2100) taxYear!: number;
  @ApiProperty({ type: [String] })
  @IsArray()
  @ArrayMinSize(12)
  @ArrayMaxSize(12)
  @Matches(TAX_DIGITS, { each: true })
  monthlyOmzet!: string[];
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateIf((o: UmkmPreviewDto) => o.firstRegisteredYear !== null)
  @IsInt()
  @Min(1990)
  @Max(2100)
  firstRegisteredYear?: number | null;
}
```

```ts
import { ApiProperty } from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { IsBoolean, Matches } from "class-validator";

export class PpnPreviewDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "2026-09-26" })
  @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/)
  date!: string;
  @ApiProperty() @Matches(/^\d{1,15}$/) amount!: string;
  @ApiProperty() @IsBoolean() luxury!: boolean;
  @ApiProperty() @IsBoolean() priceIncludesPpn!: boolean;
}

export class TaxRulesQueryDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/)
  date?: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ description: "comma-separated rule keys" })
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(/^[A-Za-z0-9.,]+$/)
  keys?: string;
}
```

`apps/api/src/tax/tax-dto-mappers.ts`:

```ts
import {
  BadRequestException,
  UnprocessableEntityException,
} from "@nestjs/common";

import { TerNotApplicableError } from "../tax-engine/ter.engine";

import type { PphOpResult } from "../tax-engine/pph-op.engine";
import type { TerYearResult } from "../tax-engine/ter.engine";
import type { UmkmResult } from "../tax-engine/umkm.engine";
import type { TaxRuleRef } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";
import type {
  PphOpResultDto,
  PpnPreviewResultDto,
  TaxRuleRefDto,
  TerPreviewResultDto,
  UmkmResultDto,
} from "@wealthme/contracts";

const s = (v: bigint) => v.toString();

export const toRuleRefDtos = (refs: readonly TaxRuleRef[]): TaxRuleRefDto[] =>
  refs.map((r) => ({ ...r }));

export function toPphOpResultDto(r: PphOpResult): PphOpResultDto {
  return {
    taxYear: r.taxYear,
    ptkp: s(r.ptkp),
    biayaJabatan: s(r.biayaJabatan),
    pensionDeduction: s(r.pensionDeduction),
    freelanceNet: s(r.freelanceNet),
    neto: s(r.neto),
    zakatApplied: s(r.zakatApplied),
    pkp: s(r.pkp),
    brackets: r.brackets.map((b) => ({
      from: s(b.from),
      upTo: b.upTo === null ? null : s(b.upTo),
      rateBps: b.rateBps,
      label: b.label,
      taxable: s(b.taxable),
      tax: s(b.tax),
    })),
    tax: s(r.tax),
    withheld: s(r.withheld),
    balance: s(r.balance),
    rules: toRuleRefDtos(r.rules),
  };
}

export function toTerResultDto(r: TerYearResult): TerPreviewResultDto {
  return {
    category: r.category,
    months: r.months.map((m) => ({
      month: m.month,
      gross: s(m.gross),
      category: m.category,
      rateBps: m.rateBps,
      withholding: s(m.withholding),
      dtp: m.dtp
        ? {
            eligible: m.dtp.eligible,
            reasons: m.dtp.reasons,
            amount: s(m.dtp.amount),
          }
        : null,
    })),
    annualTax: s(r.annualTax),
    withheldJanNov: s(r.withheldJanNov),
    december: s(r.december),
    isRefund: r.isRefund,
    dtpTotal: s(r.dtpTotal),
    rules: toRuleRefDtos(r.rules),
  };
}

export function toUmkmResultDto(
  r: UmkmResult,
  taxYear: number,
  firstRegisteredYear: number | null,
  saved: boolean,
): UmkmResultDto {
  return {
    taxYear,
    months: r.months.map((m) => ({
      month: m.month,
      omzet: s(m.omzet),
      cumulative: s(m.cumulative),
      taxable: s(m.taxable),
      tax: m.tax === null ? null : s(m.tax),
      eligible: m.eligible,
    })),
    totalOmzet: s(r.totalOmzet),
    totalTax: s(r.totalTax),
    exceedsCeiling: r.exceedsCeiling,
    ineligibleReason: r.ineligibleReason,
    firstRegisteredYear,
    saved,
    rules: toRuleRefDtos(r.rules),
  };
}

export function toPpnResultDto(r: {
  priceExcludingPpn: bigint;
  dpp: bigint;
  ppn: bigint;
  totalWithPpn: bigint;
  nominalBps: number;
  effectiveBps: number;
  rules: TaxRuleRef[];
}): PpnPreviewResultDto {
  return {
    priceExcludingPpn: s(r.priceExcludingPpn),
    dpp: s(r.dpp),
    ppn: s(r.ppn),
    totalWithPpn: s(r.totalWithPpn),
    nominalBps: r.nominalBps,
    effectiveBps: r.effectiveBps,
    rules: toRuleRefDtos(r.rules),
  };
}

export function mapEngineError(e: unknown): never {
  if (e instanceof TerNotApplicableError)
    throw new UnprocessableEntityException(e.message);
  if (e instanceof RangeError) throw new BadRequestException(e.message);
  throw e;
}
```

```ts
import { Body, Controller, HttpCode, HttpStatus, Post } from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiParam,
  ApiTags,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";

import { type AppAbility } from "../casl/casl-ability.factory";
import { CheckAbility } from "../common/decorators/check-ability.decorator";
import { PageAccess } from "../common/decorators/page-access.decorator";
import { parseDigits } from "../tax-engine/money";
import { computePphOp } from "../tax-engine/pph-op.engine";
import { terYear } from "../tax-engine/ter.engine";
import { umkmYear } from "../tax-engine/umkm.engine";

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/consistent-type-imports
import {
  PphOpPreviewDto,
  TerPreviewDto,
  UmkmPreviewDto,
} from "./dto/tax-preview.dto";
import {
  mapEngineError,
  toPphOpResultDto,
  toTerResultDto,
  toUmkmResultDto,
} from "./tax-dto-mappers";

import type { PphOpInput } from "../tax-engine/pph-op.engine";
import type {
  PphOpResultDto,
  TerPreviewResultDto,
  UmkmResultDto,
} from "@wealthme/contracts";

const opt = (v: string | undefined) => (v === undefined ? 0n : parseDigits(v));

export function pphOpInputFromDto(dto: PphOpPreviewDto): PphOpInput {
  const freelanceGross = opt(dto.freelanceGross);
  return {
    taxYear: dto.taxYear,
    status: { maritalStatus: dto.maritalStatus, dependents: dto.dependents },
    employmentGross: parseDigits(dto.employmentGross),
    pensionContribution: opt(dto.pensionContribution),
    freelance:
      freelanceGross > 0n
        ? { gross: freelanceGross, nppnBps: dto.nppnRateBps ?? 0 }
        : null,
    otherNetIncome: opt(dto.otherNetIncome),
    zakat: opt(dto.zakat),
    withheldPph21: opt(dto.withheldPph21),
  };
}

@ApiTags("tax")
@ApiBearerAuth()
@PageAccess("tax")
@Controller("family-groups/:familyGroupId/tax")
export class TaxPreviewController {
  @Post("pph-op/preview")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @CheckAbility((ab: AppAbility) => ab.can("read", "TaxRecord"))
  @ApiParam({ name: "familyGroupId", type: String })
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Preview annual PPh OP (no persistence)" })
  pphOp(@Body() dto: PphOpPreviewDto): PphOpResultDto {
    try {
      return toPphOpResultDto(computePphOp(pphOpInputFromDto(dto)));
    } catch (e) {
      return mapEngineError(e);
    }
  }

  @Post("ter/preview")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @CheckAbility((ab: AppAbility) => ab.can("read", "TaxRecord"))
  @ApiParam({ name: "familyGroupId", type: String })
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Preview PPh 21 TER Jan–Nov + December true-up + DTP flags",
  })
  ter(@Body() dto: TerPreviewDto): TerPreviewResultDto {
    try {
      return toTerResultDto(
        terYear({
          taxYear: dto.taxYear,
          status: {
            maritalStatus: dto.maritalStatus,
            dependents: dto.dependents,
          },
          monthlyGross: dto.monthlyGross.map(parseDigits),
          monthlyPension: (
            dto.monthlyPension ?? Array.from({ length: 12 }, () => "0")
          ).map(parseDigits),
          zakat: opt(dto.zakat),
          dtp: dto.dtp
            ? {
                sector: dto.dtp.sector,
                employment: dto.dtp.employment,
                dailyWage: dto.dtp.dailyWage
                  ? parseDigits(dto.dtp.dailyWage)
                  : null,
                nikValidated: dto.dtp.nikValidated,
              }
            : null,
        }),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return mapEngineError(e);
    }
  }

  @Post("umkm/preview")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @CheckAbility((ab: AppAbility) => ab.can("read", "TaxRecord"))
  @ApiParam({ name: "familyGroupId", type: String })
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Preview UMKM 0,5% final tax for a year of monthly omzet",
  })
  umkm(@Body() dto: UmkmPreviewDto): UmkmResultDto {
    try {
      const first = dto.firstRegisteredYear ?? null;
      return toUmkmResultDto(
        umkmYear({
          taxYear: dto.taxYear,
          monthlyOmzet: dto.monthlyOmzet.map(parseDigits),
          firstRegisteredYear: first,
        }),
        dto.taxYear,
        first,
        false,
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return mapEngineError(e);
    }
  }
}
```

Register it in `tax.module.ts` **before** `TaxController` (`controllers: [TaxPreviewController, TaxController]`) so static paths win over `:id`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Calculators routes**

In `calculators.controller.ts` add (keeping the existing value-import convention for DTOs):

```ts
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/consistent-type-imports
import { PpnPreviewDto, TaxRulesQueryDto } from "./dto/ppn.dto";
import { mapEngineError, toPpnResultDto, toRuleRefDtos } from "../tax/tax-dto-mappers";
import { ppnPreview } from "../tax-engine/ppn.engine";
import { TAX_RULES, ruleAt, wibDateString } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";
import type { TaxRuleKey } from "../tax-rules/tax-rule.types";
import type { PpnPreviewResultDto, TaxRuleDetailDto } from "@wealthme/contracts";

  @Get("tax-rules")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Tax rules in force on a date (static law, no family data)" })
  taxRules(@Query() q: TaxRulesQueryDto): TaxRuleDetailDto[] {
    const known = [...new Set<string>(TAX_RULES.map((e) => e.key))];
    const keys = q.keys ? q.keys.split(",").filter(Boolean) : known;
    if (keys.some((k) => !known.includes(k))) throw new BadRequestException("unknown tax rule key");
    const date = q.date ?? wibDateString(new Date());
    return (keys as TaxRuleKey[]).map((k) => {
      const r = ruleAt(k, date);
      const value: unknown = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r.value, (_key, v: unknown) => (typeof v === "bigint" ? v.toString() : v)));
      return { ...toRuleRefDtos([r.ref])[0]!, value };
    });
  }

  @Post("ppn")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "PPN information for a price (information only)" })
  ppn(@Body() dto: PpnPreviewDto): PpnPreviewResultDto {
    try {
      return toPpnResultDto(ppnPreview({ date: dto.date, amount: BigInt(dto.amount), luxury: dto.luxury, priceIncludesPpn: dto.priceIncludesPpn }));
    } catch (e) {
      return mapEngineError(e);
    }
  }
```

### Task 7: Final income — manual entries CRUD, holdings-derived estimate, crypto exchange profile

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/api/src/tax-engine/dates.ts` (+ spec case): add `wibYearStartUtc(year: number): Date`, `wibDateToUtc(date: string): Date`
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax/dto/final-income.dto.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax/final-tax.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/final-tax.service.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax/final-tax.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/investment-tax.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/final-tax.controller.spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/tax/tax.module.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `finalTaxFor`, `accruedInterest`, `FinalInstrument`, `FINAL_INSTRUMENTS`, `yearEndDate`, `wibDateString`, `toRuleRefDtos`, harness, contract types `TaxFinalIncomeEntryDto`, `CreateTaxFinalIncomeEntryDto`, `FinalTaxLineDto`, `FinalTaxEstimateDto`, `CryptoExchangeKind`.
- Produces:
  - `wibYearStartUtc(year)` = `new Date(Date.UTC(year, 0, 1) - 7h)`; `wibDateToUtc("YYYY-MM-DD")` = WIB midnight of that date as a UTC `Date`.
  - `FinalTaxService` methods (all tenant-scoped by `familyGroupId`):
    - `listEntries(familyGroupId: string, taxYear: number): Promise<TaxFinalIncomeEntryDto[]>`
    - `createEntry(familyGroupId: string, dto: CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto): Promise<TaxFinalIncomeEntryDto>`
    - `updateEntry(familyGroupId: string, id: string, dto: UpdateFinalIncomeEntryDto): Promise<TaxFinalIncomeEntryDto>`
    - `removeEntry(familyGroupId: string, id: string): Promise<void>`
    - `estimate(familyGroupId: string, taxYear: number, opts: { includeEntries: boolean }): Promise<FinalTaxEstimateDto>`
    - `setCryptoExchange(familyGroupId: string, investmentId: string, kind: CryptoExchangeKind | null): Promise<{ investmentId: string; cryptoExchange: CryptoExchangeKind | null }>`
  - Routes:
    - `@PageAccess("tax")` `family-groups/:familyGroupId/tax`:
      - `GET final-income?year=` (read TaxRecord)
      - `POST final-income` (manage)
      - `PATCH final-income/:entryId` (manage)
      - `DELETE final-income/:entryId` (manage, 204)
      - `GET final-tax?year=` → holdings + entries (read)
    - `@PageAccess("investments")` `family-groups/:familyGroupId/investment-tax`:
      - `GET estimate?year=` → holdings only, no manual entries (read Investment). This is what /investasi and /laporan use; it exposes nothing beyond holding data those pages already show.
      - `PATCH :investmentId/profile` `{ cryptoExchange: "domestik" | "luar_negeri" | null }` (manage Investment; 400 unless the investment is this family's `kripto`).

**Holdings → lines (Key decision 10), per holding with `currency === "IDR"` (others increment `skippedForeign`):**

| `type`       | instrument                                                                  | gross                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | rule date                  | caveat                                                                                                                      |
| ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `deposito`   | `bunga_deposito`                                                            | `accruedInterest(principal = quantity × costBasisPerUnit, rate = interestRatePct × 100, from = purchasedAt WIB date ?? "YYYY-01-01", to = maturityDate WIB date ?? null)`; `principal` also passed for the Rp 7,5 jt threshold | `yearEndDate(taxYear)`     | `no_interest_rate` (tax `null`, gross `"0"`) if `interestRatePct` is null; else `no_purchase_date` if `purchasedAt` is null |
| `obligasi`   | `kupon_sbn`                                                                 | same accrual as deposito, no principal threshold                                                                                                                                                                               | `yearEndDate(taxYear)`     | `no_interest_rate` / `no_purchase_date` / `assumed_sbn` (first that applies)                                                |
| `saham`      | `saham_idx`                                                                 | Σ sell `quantity × pricePerUnit` in the tax year, rounded half-up to rupiah                                                                                                                                                    | last sell's WIB date       | —                                                                                                                           |
| `kripto`     | `kripto_luar` if `cryptoExchange === "luar_negeri"`, else `kripto_domestik` | Σ sells **grouped by the rule entry in force on each sell's WIB date** (one line per group)                                                                                                                                    | the group's last sell date | `assumed_domestic` when `cryptoExchange` is null                                                                            |
| `reksa_dana` | `reksa_dana`                                                                | Σ sells                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | last sell date             | —                                                                                                                           |
| `emas`       | `emas`                                                                      | Σ sells                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | last sell date             | — (rule rate `null` → `rateSource: "missing"`)                                                                              |

Holdings with zero accrued interest and no sells produce no line. Decimal products use Prisma `Decimal` (`times`, then `toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP)`), never `Number`.

`apps/api/src/tax/final-tax.service.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestException, NotFoundException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { Decimal } from "@prisma/client/runtime/library";

import { FinalTaxService } from "./final-tax.service";

const FG = "fg-1";
const d = (v: string) => new Decimal(v);
const at = (date: string) => new Date(`${date}T03:00:00.000Z`); // 10:00 WIB

function inv(o: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return {
    id: "inv",
    name: "Holding",
    ticker: "",
    type: "saham",
    quantity: d("0"),
    costBasisPerUnit: d("0"),
    currency: "IDR",
    interestRatePct: null,
    purchasedAt: null,
    maturityDate: null,
    cryptoExchange: null,
    transactions: [],
    ...o,
  };
}

const prisma = {
  investment: { findMany: jest.fn(), findFirst: jest.fn(), update: jest.fn() },
  account: { findFirst: jest.fn() },
  taxFinalIncomeEntry: {
    findMany: jest.fn(),
    findFirst: jest.fn(),
    create: jest.fn(),
    update: jest.fn(),
    delete: jest.fn(),
  },
};

describe("FinalTaxService.estimate", () => {
  const svc = new FinalTaxService(prisma as never);
  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.resetAllMocks();
    prisma.taxFinalIncomeEntry.findMany.mockResolvedValue([]);
  });

  it("derives deposito, SBN, saham lines with golden numbers and skips foreign holdings", async () => {
    prisma.investment.findMany.mockResolvedValue([
      inv({
        id: "dep",
        type: "deposito",
        quantity: d("1"),
        costBasisPerUnit: d("100000000.00"),
        interestRatePct: d("4.25"),
        purchasedAt: at("2026-03-01"),
        maturityDate: at("2027-03-01"),
      }),
      inv({

        costBasisPerUnit: d("1000000.00"),
        interestRatePct: d("6.40"),

      inv({
        id: "bbca",
        type: "saham",
        ticker: "BBCA",
        transactions: [
          {
            quantity: d("1000"),
            pricePerUnit: d("9250.00"),
            occurredAt: at("2026-05-04"),
          },
        ],
      }),
      inv({
        id: "aapl",
        type: "saham",
        currency: "USD",
        transactions: [
          {
            quantity: d("1"),
            pricePerUnit: d("200.00"),
            occurredAt: at("2026-05-04"),
          },
        ],
      }),

    const r = await svc.estimate(FG, 2026, { includeEntries: false });
    expect(
      r.lines.map((l) => [

        l.tax,

    ).toEqual([

      ["sbn", "kupon_sbn", "3200000", "320000", "assumed_sbn"],
      ["bbca", "saham_idx", "9250000", "9250", null],
    ]);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({
      totalTax: "1041853",
      missingRateCount: 0,
      skippedForeign: 1,
    });
    expect(prisma.investment.findMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({
        where: expect.objectContaining({ familyGroupId: FG }),
      }),

  });

  it("splits kripto sells at 2025-08-01 and marks the domestic assumption", async () => {
    prisma.investment.findMany.mockResolvedValue([
      inv({
        id: "btc",
        type: "kripto",
        transactions: [
          {
            quantity: d("0.5"),
            pricePerUnit: d("100000000.00"),
            occurredAt: at("2025-07-31"),
          },
          {
            quantity: d("0.5"),
            pricePerUnit: d("100000000.00"),
            occurredAt: at("2025-08-01"),
          },

  });

  it("foreign crypto exchange uses 1%; emas sells have no rate", async () => {
    prisma.investment.findMany.mockResolvedValue([
      inv({
        id: "eth",
        type: "kripto",
        cryptoExchange: "luar_negeri",
        transactions: [
          {
            quantity: d("1"),
            pricePerUnit: d("50000000.00"),
            occurredAt: at("2026-02-02"),
          },
        ],
      }),
      inv({
        id: "au",
        type: "emas",
        transactions: [
          {
            quantity: d("10"),
            pricePerUnit: d("1500000.00"),
            occurredAt: at("2026-02-02"),

        ],
      }),
    ]);
    const r = await svc.estimate(FG, 2026, { includeEntries: false });
    expect(r.lines.map((l) => [l.instrument, l.tax, l.rateSource])).toEqual([
      ["kripto_luar", "500000", "rule"],
      ["emas", null, "missing"],
    ]);
    expect(r.missingRateCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it("deposito without an interest rate yields a no_interest_rate line with tax null", async () => {
    prisma.investment.findMany.mockResolvedValue([
      inv({
        id: "dep",
        type: "deposito",

      }),

    const r = await svc.estimate(FG, 2026, { includeEntries: false });
    expect(r.lines[0]).toMatchObject({
      caveat: "no_interest_rate",
      tax: null,
      grossAmount: "0",
    });
  });

  it("adds manual entries: dividen 10% (0% reinvested), P2P with override", async () => {
    prisma.investment.findMany.mockResolvedValue([]);
    prisma.taxFinalIncomeEntry.findMany.mockResolvedValue([
      {
        id: "e1",
        taxYear: 2026,
        instrument: "dividen",
        grossAmount: 2_000_000n,
        occurredAt: at("2026-05-04"),
        reinvested: false,
        rateBpsOverride: null,
        investmentId: null,
        accountId: null,
        notes: "BBRI",
      },
      {
        id: "e2",
        taxYear: 2026,
        instrument: "dividen",
        grossAmount: 2_000_000n,
        occurredAt: at("2026-05-04"),
        reinvested: true,
        rateBpsOverride: null,
        investmentId: null,
        accountId: null,
        notes: null,
      },
      {
        id: "e3",
        taxYear: 2026,
        instrument: "p2p",
        grossAmount: 1_000_000n,
        occurredAt: at("2026-06-01"),
        reinvested: false,
        rateBpsOverride: 1500,
        investmentId: null,
        accountId: null,
        notes: null,
      },
    ]);
    const r = await svc.estimate(FG, 2026, { includeEntries: true });
    expect(r.lines.map((l) => [l.entryId, l.tax, l.rateSource])).toEqual([
      ["e1", "200000", "rule"],
      ["e2", "0", "rule"],
      ["e3", "150000", "override"],
    ]);
    expect(r.totalTax).toBe("350000");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional, PartialType } from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { Type } from "class-transformer";
import {
  IsBoolean,
  IsIn,
  IsInt,
  IsOptional,
  IsString,
  IsUUID,
  Matches,
  Max,
  MaxLength,
  Min,
  ValidateIf,
} from "class-validator";

import { FINAL_INSTRUMENTS } from "../../tax-engine/final-tax.engine";

import { TAX_DIGITS } from "./tax-preview.dto";

import type { FinalInstrument } from "../../tax-engine/final-tax.engine";

export class CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto {
  @ApiProperty({ enum: FINAL_INSTRUMENTS })
  @IsIn(FINAL_INSTRUMENTS)
  instrument!: FinalInstrument;
  @ApiProperty() @Matches(TAX_DIGITS) grossAmount!: string;
  @ApiProperty({ example: "2026-05-04" })
  @Matches(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/)
  occurredAt!: string;
  @ApiPropertyOptional() @IsOptional() @IsBoolean() reinvested?: boolean;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateIf((o: CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto) => o.rateBpsOverride !== null)
  @IsInt()
  @Min(0)
  @Max(10_000)
  rateBpsOverride?: number | null;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateIf((o: CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto) => o.investmentId !== null)
  @IsUUID()
  investmentId?: string | null;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateIf((o: CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto) => o.accountId !== null)
  @IsUUID()
  accountId?: string | null;
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateIf((o: CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto) => o.notes !== null)
  @IsString()
  @MaxLength(200)
  notes?: string | null;
}
export class UpdateFinalIncomeEntryDto extends PartialType(
  CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto,
) {}

export class TaxYearQueryDto {
  @ApiProperty()
  @Type(() => Number)
  @IsInt()
  @Min(2000)
  @Max(2100)
  year!: number;
}

export class CryptoExchangeProfileDto {
  @ApiProperty({ enum: ["domestik", "luar_negeri"], nullable: true })
  @ValidateIf((o: CryptoExchangeProfileDto) => o.cryptoExchange !== null)
  @IsIn(["domestik", "luar_negeri"])
  cryptoExchange!: "domestik" | "luar_negeri" | null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `final-tax.service.ts`**

Key parts (write the whole class; helper names are binding):

```ts
import {
  BadRequestException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { Decimal } from "@prisma/client/runtime/library";

import { wibDateToUtc, wibYearStartUtc } from "../tax-engine/dates";
import { accruedInterest, finalTaxFor } from "../tax-engine/final-tax.engine";
import { wibDateString, yearEndDate } from "../tax-rules/tax-rules.registry";
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/consistent-type-imports
import { PrismaService } from "../prisma/prisma.service";

import { toRuleRefDtos } from "./tax-dto-mappers";

import type {
  CreateFinalIncomeEntryDto,
  UpdateFinalIncomeEntryDto,
} from "./dto/final-income.dto";
import type { FinalInstrument } from "../tax-engine/final-tax.engine";
import type {
  CryptoExchangeKind,
  FinalTaxEstimateDto,
  FinalTaxLineDto,
  TaxFinalIncomeEntryDto,
} from "@wealthme/contracts";

const toRupiah = (v: Decimal) =>
  BigInt(v.toDecimalPlaces(0, Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP).toFixed(0));
const TAXED_TYPES = [
  "deposito",
  "obligasi",
  "saham",
  "kripto",
  "reksa_dana",
  "emas",
] as const;
const SELL_INSTRUMENT = {
  saham: "saham_idx",
  reksa_dana: "reksa_dana",
  emas: "emas",
} as const;

function line(
  base: Pick<
    FinalTaxLineDto,
    | "source"
    | "instrument"
    | "label"
    | "investmentId"
    | "accountId"
    | "entryId"
    | "caveat"
  >,
  gross: bigint,
  calc: ReturnType<typeof finalTaxFor> | null,
): FinalTaxLineDto {
  return {
    ...base,
    grossAmount: gross.toString(),
    rateBps: calc?.rateBps ?? null,
    tax: calc?.tax == null ? null : calc.tax.toString(),
    rateSource: calc?.rateSource ?? "rule",
    belowThreshold: calc?.belowThreshold ?? false,
    rule: toRuleRefDtos([calc!.ref])[0]!,
  };
}
```

For the `no_interest_rate` case, still call `finalTaxFor({ instrument, grossAmount: 0n, date })` to get the `ref`, then override `tax` to `null` in the line (`{ ...line(...), tax: null }`).

- `holdingLines(familyGroupId, taxYear)`:
  - Query `prisma.investment.findMany({ where: { familyGroupId, type: { in: [...TAXED_TYPES] } }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" }, select: { id, name, ticker, type, quantity, costBasisPerUnit, currency, interestRatePct, purchasedAt, maturityDate, cryptoExchange, transactions: { where: { type: "sell", occurredAt: { gte: wibYearStartUtc(taxYear), lt: wibYearStartUtc(taxYear + 1) } }, orderBy: { occurredAt: "asc" }, select: { quantity, pricePerUnit, occurredAt } } } })`.
  - Apply the table above. `rateHundredths = BigInt(interestRatePct.times(100).toFixed(0))`.
  - Kripto grouping key: `finalTaxFor({ instrument, grossAmount: 0n, date }).ref.effectiveFrom`.
  - The label is `ticker ? \`${ticker} · ${name}\` : name`.
- `entryLines(familyGroupId, taxYear)`: `taxFinalIncomeEntry.findMany({ where: { familyGroupId, taxYear }, orderBy: { occurredAt: "asc" } })`. Each entry goes through `finalTaxFor({ instrument, grossAmount, date: wibDateString(occurredAt), reinvested, rateBpsOverride })`, with `source: "entry"`, `label: notes ?? ""`, `caveat: null`.
- `estimate`: concatenate holding lines, then entry lines when `includeEntries`. `totalTax` = sum of non-null taxes. `missingRateCount` = lines with `tax === null`.
- `createEntry`:
  - `reinvested === true` with an instrument other than `dividen` → 400.
  - Validate `investmentId`/`accountId` with `findFirst({ where: { id, familyGroupId }, select: { id: true } })` → 400 when null.
  - `taxYear = Number(occurredAt.slice(0, 4))`; persist `occurredAt: wibDateToUtc(occurredAt)`.
- `updateEntry` / `removeEntry`: first `findFirst({ where: { id, familyGroupId } })` → 404 when null. Then `update`/`delete` with `where: { id }`. `updateEntry` re-runs the same validations on the merged record.
- `toEntryDto`: `occurredAt: wibDateString(row.occurredAt)`, `grossAmount: row.grossAmount.toString()`.
- `setCryptoExchange`: `investment.findFirst({ where: { id, familyGroupId }, select: { id: true, type: true } })`. Missing or not `kripto` → 400. Then `investment.update({ where: { id }, data: { cryptoExchange: kind } })`.

Add to `dates.ts`:

```ts
const WIB_MS = 7 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
export const wibYearStartUtc = (year: number) =>
  new Date(Date.UTC(year, 0, 1) - WIB_MS);
export const wibDateToUtc = (date: string) =>
  new Date(Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`) - WIB_MS);
```

and a `dates.spec.ts` case: `wibDateToUtc("2026-01-01").toISOString() === "2025-12-31T17:00:00.000Z"`.

### Task 8: Detailed TaxRecord mode, UMKM year storage, annual summary

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/api/src/tax/tax.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/dto/create-tax-record.dto.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/dto/update-tax-record.dto.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/dto/tax-record-response.dto.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/tax.controller.spec.ts` (fixture only: add legacy defaults to `taxRow`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax/tax.service.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax/umkm-year.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/tax-summary.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/tax-summary.service.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/tax/tax-year.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/tax/tax-year.controller.spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/tax/tax.module.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `computePphOp`, `toRuleRefDtos`, `umkmYear`, `toUmkmResultDto`, `sptTiming`, `FinalTaxService.estimate`, `wibDateString`, `uniqueRefs`, `TAX_DIGITS`, `MARITAL`.
- Produces:
  - `TaxService.computeRecord(r: RecordInputs): { netTax: bigint; ptkp: bigint; rules: TaxRuleRef[] }`, where `RecordInputs = { taxYear; calcMode: "legacy" | "detailed"; grossIncome: bigint; ptkp: bigint; zakat: bigint; maritalStatus: MaritalStatus | null; dependents: number | null; pensionContribution: bigint | null; freelanceGross: bigint | null; nppnRateBps: number | null; otherNetIncome: bigint | null; withheldPph21: bigint | null }`
  - `TaxService.findByYear(familyGroupId: string, taxYear: number): Promise<TaxRecordDto | null>`
  - `UmkmYearService.get(familyGroupId, taxYear): Promise<UmkmResultDto | null>`, `UmkmYearService.put(familyGroupId, taxYear, dto: UmkmYearPutDto): Promise<UmkmResultDto>`
  - `TaxSummaryService.summary(familyGroupId: string, taxYear: number, today?: string): Promise<TaxAnnualSummaryDto>`
  - Routes (`@PageAccess("tax")`, `family-groups/:familyGroupId/tax`, controller registered before `TaxController`):
    - `GET umkm/:year` → `UmkmResultDto | null` (read)
    - `PUT umkm/:year` → `UmkmResultDto` with `saved: true` (manage)
    - `GET summary?year=` → `TaxAnnualSummaryDto` (read)
  - `TaxRecordDto` responses now carry the Task 5 fields.

**Rules for writes:**

1. **Mode.**
   - A POST with `maritalStatus` → `calcMode: detailed`; `ptkp` is derived by the engine and stored (client `ptkp` ignored). A POST without `maritalStatus` requires `ptkp` (400 otherwise) and stays `legacy`.
   - A PATCH with `maritalStatus` switches the row to `detailed`. A PATCH on a detailed row merges the provided detailed fields over the stored ones. A PATCH on a legacy row without `maritalStatus` keeps it legacy and recomputes with the legacy formula (unchanged behaviour).
2. **Detailed inputs.** `grossIncome` is the employment gross (`"0"` for pure freelancers). `freelanceGross > 0` requires `nppnRateBps`. `zakat` still comes only from `DonationsService.getTotalDeductibleAmount` (server-authoritative, as today).
3. **`sptFiledAt`.** Set to `now` when the written `sptStatus` becomes `sudah_lapor` and the stored one was not. Cleared to `null` when it moves away from `sudah_lapor`. Otherwise unchanged.
4. **`balance`.** `netTax − (withheldPph21 ?? 0)`.
5. **Server-authoritative.** The client-sent `netTax` stays ignored.

```ts
  id: "t1",

  dependents: null,

});

    findMany: jest.fn(),

const echo = ({ create, data }: { create?: object; data?: object }) =>

describe("TaxService — legacy rows are unchanged", () => {

  beforeEach(() => {

      sptFiledAt: null,
    });
    expect(r!.rules.map((x) => x.key)).toContain("pph.pasal17");
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 5: UMKM year service + summary service**

`umkm-year.service.ts`:

```ts
@Injectable()
export class UmkmYearService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  async get(
    familyGroupId: string,
    taxYear: number,
  ): Promise<UmkmResultDto | null> {
    const row = await this.prisma.taxUmkmYear.findUnique({
      where: { familyGroupId_taxYear: { familyGroupId, taxYear } },
    });
    if (!row) return null;
    return toUmkmResultDto(
      umkmYear({
        taxYear,
        monthlyOmzet: row.omzetByMonth,
        firstRegisteredYear: row.firstRegisteredYear,
      }),
      taxYear,
      row.firstRegisteredYear,
      true,
    );
  }

  async put(
    familyGroupId: string,
    taxYear: number,
    dto: UmkmYearPutDto,
  ): Promise<UmkmResultDto> {
    const omzet = dto.monthlyOmzet.map(parseDigits);
    const first = dto.firstRegisteredYear ?? null;
    const result = umkmYear({
      taxYear,
      monthlyOmzet: omzet,
      firstRegisteredYear: first,
    });
    await this.prisma.taxUmkmYear.upsert({
      where: { familyGroupId_taxYear: { familyGroupId, taxYear } },
      create: {
        familyGroupId,
        taxYear,
        omzetByMonth: omzet,
        firstRegisteredYear: first,
      },
      update: { omzetByMonth: omzet, firstRegisteredYear: first },
    });
    return toUmkmResultDto(result, taxYear, first, true);
  }
}
```

`UmkmYearPutDto` (in `tax-preview.dto.ts`) = `UmkmPreviewDto` without `taxYear` (the year comes from the path, `ParseIntPipe` + range check 2000–2100 → 400).

`tax-summary.service.ts`:

```ts
@Injectable()
export class TaxSummaryService {
  constructor(
    private readonly tax: TaxService,
    private readonly finalTax: FinalTaxService,
    private readonly umkm: UmkmYearService,
  ) {}

  async summary(
    familyGroupId: string,
    taxYear: number,
    today = wibDateString(new Date()),
  ): Promise<TaxAnnualSummaryDto> {
    const [record, finalTax, umkm] = await Promise.all([
      this.tax.findByYear(familyGroupId, taxYear),
      this.finalTax.estimate(familyGroupId, taxYear, { includeEntries: true }),
      this.umkm.get(familyGroupId, taxYear),
    ]);
    const timing = sptTiming(taxYear, today);
    const total =
      BigInt(record?.netTax ?? "0") +
      BigInt(finalTax.totalTax) +
      BigInt(umkm?.totalTax ?? "0");
    const rules = [
      ...(record?.rules ?? []),
      ...finalTax.lines.map((l) => l.rule),
      ...(umkm?.rules ?? []),
      ...toRuleRefDtos(timing.rules),
    ];
    return {
      taxYear,
      pphOp: record
        ? {
            recordId: record.id,
            calcMode: record.calcMode,
            tax: record.netTax,
            withheld: record.withheldPph21 ?? "0",
            balance: record.balance,
          }
        : null,
      finalTax: {
        totalTax: finalTax.totalTax,
        lineCount: finalTax.lines.length,
        missingRateCount: finalTax.missingRateCount,
      },
      umkm: umkm
        ? {
            totalTax: umkm.totalTax,
            exceedsCeiling: umkm.exceedsCeiling,
            ineligibleReason: umkm.ineligibleReason,
          }
        : null,
      totalEstimatedTax: total.toString(),
      spt: {
        status: record?.sptStatus ?? null,
        deadline: timing.deadline,
        graceUntil: timing.graceUntil,
        daysToDeadline: timing.daysToDeadline,
        pastDeadline: timing.pastDeadline,
        withinGrace: timing.withinGrace,
        filedAt: record?.sptFiledAt ?? null,
      },
      rules: [
        ...new Map(
          rules.map((r) => [`${r.key}|${r.effectiveFrom}`, r]),
        ).values(),
      ],
    };
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `format-bps.ts`, `tax-rule-badge.tsx`, `tax-query-keys.ts`, `use-tax-api.ts`**

```ts
// format-bps.ts
export function formatBps(bps: number | null): string {
  if (bps === null) return "—";
  const whole = Math.trunc(bps / 100);
  const frac = String(bps % 100)
    .padStart(2, "0")
    .replace(/0+$/, "");
  return frac ? `${whole},${frac}%` : `${whole}%`;
}
```

```tsx
// tax-rule-badge.tsx
"use client";
import { useLocale } from "@/hooks/use-locale";

import type { TaxRuleRefDto } from "@wealthme/contracts";

export function TaxRuleBadge({ rules }: { rules: readonly TaxRuleRefDto[] }) {
  const { t } = useLocale();
  const flagged = rules.filter((r) => !r.verified || r.outOfRange);
  if (flagged.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <details className="group inline-block text-[11px]">
      <summary className="badge badge--warning cursor-pointer list-none">
        {t.tax.rules.unverifiedBadge}
      </summary>
      <ul className="mt-1.5 space-y-1 rounded-[10px] border border-warning/30 bg-card p-2.5 text-muted-foreground shadow-sm">
        {flagged.map((r) => (
          <li key={`${r.key}|${r.effectiveFrom}`}>
            {r.source ? (
              <a
                href={r.source}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
              >
                {r.legalBasis}
              </a>
            ) : (
              <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
                {r.legalBasis}
              </span>
            )}
            {r.outOfRange && <span> · {t.tax.rules.outOfRange}</span>}
            {r.note && <span className="block">{r.note}</span>}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </details>
  );
}
```

```ts
// tax-query-keys.ts — local on purpose: @/lib/query-keys is a protected file.
// Every key starts with ["tax", fgId] so the existing queryKeys.tax.list(fgId) invalidation refreshes them too.
export const taxKeys = {
  pphOpPreview: (fg: string, req: unknown) =>
    ["tax", fg, "pph-op-preview", req] as const,
  terPreview: (fg: string, req: unknown) =>
    ["tax", fg, "ter-preview", req] as const,
  umkmPreview: (fg: string, req: unknown) =>
    ["tax", fg, "umkm-preview", req] as const,
  umkmYear: (fg: string, year: number) => ["tax", fg, "umkm", year] as const,
  finalTax: (fg: string, year: number) =>
    ["tax", fg, "final-tax", year] as const,
  finalIncome: (fg: string, year: number) =>
    ["tax", fg, "final-income", year] as const,
  summary: (fg: string, year: number) => ["tax", fg, "summary", year] as const,
  investmentTax: (fg: string, year: number) =>
    ["investment-tax", fg, year] as const,
  rules: (date: string, keys: readonly string[]) =>
    ["tax-rules", date, keys] as const,
  ppn: (req: unknown) => ["ppn-preview", req] as const,
};
```

`use-tax-api.ts` wraps `apiFetch` from `@/lib/api-client` with TanStack Query, the same way the /tax page does today:

- The session comes from `useSession()` of `@/lib/auth-compat` (`session?.accessToken`, `session?.familyGroupId ?? null`).
- Previews are `useQuery` with `placeholderData: keepPreviousData`, `enabled: !!token && !!fg && req !== null`, `method: "POST"`.
- Mutations invalidate `["tax", fg]` (and `["investment-tax", fg]` for `useSetCryptoExchange`).
- `useTaxRules` calls `GET /calculators/tax-rules?date=…&keys=a,b` with `enabled: true` (public).
- `usePpnPreview` calls `POST /calculators/ppn` (public).

Every hook returns the TanStack result object unchanged. The web never recomputes a tax number.

2. Replace the `ptkp` state with `const [maritalStatus, setMaritalStatus] = useState<TaxMaritalStatus>("TK")` and `const [dependents, setDependents] = useState(0)`. Add string states `pension`, `withheld`, `freelance`, `nppnPct` (a percent string, e.g. `"50"`), `otherNet`. Debounce them like `gross`.
3. Replace the `/calculators/pph21` query with:

```ts
const pphOpRequest: PphOpPreviewRequestDto = {
  taxYear,
  maritalStatus,
  dependents,
  employmentGross: toClampedDigitString(debouncedGross, MAX_TAX_INPUT),
  pensionContribution: toClampedDigitString(debouncedPension, MAX_TAX_INPUT),
  zakat: toClampedDigitString(debouncedZakat, MAX_TAX_INPUT),
  withheldPph21: toClampedDigitString(debouncedWithheld, MAX_TAX_INPUT),
  otherNetIncome: toClampedDigitString(debouncedOtherNet, MAX_TAX_INPUT),
  ...(toClampedDigitString(debouncedFreelance, MAX_TAX_INPUT) !== "0"
    ? {
        freelanceGross: toClampedDigitString(debouncedFreelance, MAX_TAX_INPUT),
        nppnRateBps:
          Math.round(Number(debouncedNppnPct.replace(",", ".")) * 100) || 0,
      }
    : {}),
};
const {
  data: pphOp,
  isFetching: isPph21Fetching,
  isError: isPph21Error,
} = usePphOpPreview(fgId ? pphOpRequest : null);
```

(`nppnRateBps: 0` makes the server answer 400, so the panel shows `t.tax.calcErrorLabel` until the user types a valid NPPN %. `_components/ter-panel.tsx` — `export function TerPanel({ taxYear, maritalStatus, dependents, defaultMonthlyGross }: { taxYear: number; maritalStatus: TaxMaritalStatus; dependents: number; defaultMonthlyGross: string })`:

- Status and dependents come from the calculator above (one source of truth). The monthly gross defaults to the annual gross ÷ 12, computed with BigInt on the digit string (`(BigInt(annual) / 12n).toString()`) as a starting value only. The user can open "Atur per bulan" to edit each of the 12 months.
- DTP fieldset: sector select (with `none` → `null`), employment radio, daily wage input (shown only when non-permanent), NIK checkbox.
- Calls `useTerPreview({ taxYear, maritalStatus, dependents, monthlyGross, dtp })`.
- Renders `.table table--num` with 12 rows. December's label is `t.tax.ter.decemberLabel` and its rate is `—`. A negative December is shown as `(Rp 2.000.000)` in `text-success` with `t.tax.ter.refundNote`. The DTP column shows the amount or the translated first reason. Footer: `t.tax.ter.dtpTotalPrefix` + `dtpTotal`. Then `<TaxRuleBadge rules={result.rules}/>`. The `t.tax.ter.kiNote` notice appears when `maritalStatus === "KI"`.
- HTTP 422 from the API → the notice `t.tax.ter.notApplicable` instead of the table.

Mount it in `page.tsx` directly below the progressive-breakdown `SectionCard`, inside its own `SectionCard title={t.tax.ter.title}`.

`_components/ter-panel.test.tsx` (mock `apiFetch` like `tax.test.tsx`):

```tsx
it("renders the December refund from the server as a positive amount in parentheses with the refund note", async () => {
  mockApiFetch.mockImplementation((url: string) => {
    if (url.endsWith("/tax/ter/preview")) {
      return Promise.resolve({
        category: "A",
        annualTax: "9000000",
        withheldJanNov: "11000000",
        december: "-2000000",
        isRefund: true,
        dtpTotal: "0",
        rules: [],
        months: [
          ...Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => ({
            month: i + 1,
            gross: i === 0 ? "50000000" : "10000000",
            category: "A",
            rateBps: i === 0 ? 1800 : 200,
            withholding: i === 0 ? "9000000" : "200000",
            dtp: null,
          })),
          {
            month: 12,
            gross: "10000000",
            category: "A",
            rateBps: null,
            withholding: "-2000000",
            dtp: null,
          },
        ],
      });
    }
    return Promise.resolve(null);
  });
  render(
    <TerPanel
      taxYear={2026}
      maritalStatus="TK"
      dependents={0}
      defaultMonthlyGross="10000000"
    />,
  );
  expect(await screen.findByText(/Lebih potong/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText("(Rp 2.000.000)")).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText("18%")).toBeInTheDocument();
});
it("shows the TER-not-applicable notice on 422", async () => {
  mockApiFetch.mockRejectedValue({
    status: 422,
    message: "TER berlaku mulai 2024 (tahun pajak 2023)",
  });
  render(
    <TerPanel
      taxYear={2023}
      maritalStatus="TK"
      dependents={0}
      defaultMonthlyGross="10000000"
    />,
  );
  expect(
    await screen.findByText("TER berlaku mulai tahun pajak 2024."),
  ).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.queryByRole("table")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
});
it("K/I shows the husband-category note", async () => {
  mockApiFetch.mockResolvedValue(null);
  render(
    <TerPanel
      taxYear={2026}
      maritalStatus="KI"
      dependents={1}
      defaultMonthlyGross="10000000"
    />,
  );
  expect(
    await screen.findByText(
      "Untuk K/I, pemotongan bulanan memakai kategori K/n suami.",
    ),
  ).toBeInTheDocument();
});
```

(Use the project's `formatRupiah` output format when asserting `"(Rp 2.000.000)"`; if it renders `Rp2.000.000` without a space, assert that exact string.)

| `final.belowThreshold`                                                                                        | Pokok ≤ Rp7,5 jt — dikecualikan                                                                                                                                                                                          | Principal ≤ Rp7.5m — exempt                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `final.missingRate`                                                                                           | Tarif belum terverifikasi — isi sendiri                                                                                                                                                                                  | Rate not verified — enter your own                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `final.missingRateNotice`                                                                                     | {n} baris belum punya tarif.                                                                                                                                                                                             | {n} line(s) have no rate yet.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `final.skippedForeign`                                                                                        | {n} aset dalam mata uang asing tidak dihitung (pajak Indonesia memakai kurs pajak KMK).                                                                                                                                  | {n} foreign-currency holding(s) not estimated (Indonesian tax uses the KMK tax rate).                                                                                                                                  |
| `final.addEntry` / `editEntry` / `deleteEntry`                                                                | Tambah penghasilan / Ubah / Hapus                                                                                                                                                                                        | Add income / Edit / Delete                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `final.fieldInstrument` / `fieldGross` / `fieldDate` / `fieldReinvested` / `fieldRateOverride` / `fieldNotes` | Jenis / Jumlah bruto / Tanggal diterima / Diinvestasikan kembali di Indonesia ≤ 3 tahun / Tarif sendiri (%) — opsional / Catatan                                                                                         | Type / Gross amount / Date received / Reinvested in Indonesia within 3 years / Own rate (%) — optional / Notes                                                                                                         |
| `final.totalPrefix`                                                                                           | Total perkiraan pajak final:                                                                                                                                                                                             | Estimated final tax total:                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `final.toastSaved` / `toastDeleted` / `toastFailed`                                                           | Tersimpan / Dihapus / Gagal menyimpan                                                                                                                                                                                    | Saved / Deleted / Save failed                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `summary.title`                                                                                               | Ringkasan pajak tahunan                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Annual tax summary                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `summary.totalLabel`                                                                                          | Perkiraan total pajak tahun {year}                                                                                                                                                                                       | Estimated total tax for {year}                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `summary.pphOpLabel` / `finalLabel` / `umkmLabel`                                                             | PPh orang pribadi / Pajak final / PPh final UMKM                                                                                                                                                                         | Personal income tax / Final tax / UMKM final tax                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `summary.noRecord`                                                                                            | Belum ada catatan PPh — hitung dan simpan di kalkulator di atas.                                                                                                                                                         | No income-tax record yet — calculate and save it above.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `summary.balanceDue` / `balanceOver`                                                                          | Kurang bayar / Lebih bayar                                                                                                                                                                                               | Underpaid / Overpaid                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `summary.deadlineLabel`                                                                                       | Batas lapor SPT Tahunan                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Annual SPT deadline                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `summary.daysLeft` / `summary.daysLate`                                                                       | {n} hari lagi / lewat {n} hari                                                                                                                                                                                           | {n} days left / {n} days past                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `summary.graceNote`                                                                                           | Sanksi administrasi dihapus bila lapor/bayar s.d. {date} (pengumuman DJP terkait Coretax) — batas formal tetap {deadline}.                                                                                               | Administrative penalties waived if filed/paid by {date} (DJP announcement on Coretax) — the formal deadline stays {deadline}.                                                                                          |
| `summary.coretaxNote`                                                                                         | Lapor lewat Coretax DJP (coretaxdjp.pajak.go.id). NIK berfungsi sebagai NPWP.                                                                                                                                            | File through DJP Coretax (coretaxdjp.pajak.go.id). Your NIK serves as your NPWP.                                                                                                                                       |
| `summary.filedAtPrefix`                                                                                       | Dilaporkan:                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Filed:                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

```tsx
it("shows server months, the ceiling warning and saves 12 digit strings via PUT", async () => {
  mockApiFetch.mockImplementation(
    (url: string, init?: { method?: string; body?: string }) => {
      if (url.endsWith("/tax/umkm/2026") && !init?.method)
        return Promise.resolve(null);
      if (url.endsWith("/tax/umkm/preview"))
        return Promise.resolve(
          umkmResult({ totalTax: "21500000", exceedsCeiling: true }),
        );
      if (url.endsWith("/tax/umkm/2026") && init?.method === "PUT")
        return Promise.resolve(umkmResult({ saved: true }));
      return Promise.resolve(null);
    },
  );
  render(<UmkmPanel taxYear={2026} canManage />);
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Jan"), "400000000");
  expect(await screen.findByText(/Omzet melewati Rp4,8 M/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText("Rp 21.500.000")).toBeInTheDocument();
  await userEvent.click(
    screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Simpan omzet tahun ini" }),
  );
  const put = mockApiFetch.mock.calls.find(
    ([u, i]) => u.endsWith("/tax/umkm/2026") && i?.method === "PUT",
  );
  expect(JSON.parse(put[1].body).monthlyOmzet).toHaveLength(12);
  expect(JSON.parse(put[1].body).monthlyOmzet[0]).toBe("400000000");
});
it("an ineligible month renders the Pasal 17 note instead of a tax", async () => {
  const r = umkmResult({ ineligibleReason: "time_limit" });
  r.months[0] = { ...r.months[0]!, eligible: false, tax: null };
  mockApiFetch.mockImplementation((url: string) =>
    Promise.resolve(url.endsWith("/tax/umkm/preview") ? r : null),
  );
  render(<UmkmPanel taxYear={2026} canManage />);
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText("Jan"), "70000000");
  expect(
    await screen.findByText("Lewat batas 7 tahun — gunakan tarif Pasal 17"),
  ).toBeInTheDocument();
});
it("canManage=false disables Save", async () => {
  mockApiFetch.mockResolvedValue(null);
  render(<UmkmPanel taxYear={2026} canManage={false} />);
  expect(
    screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Simpan omzet tahun ini" }),
  ).toBeDisabled();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the three components + dialog**

- **`UmkmPanel`.**
  - State: `omzet: string[12]` (formatted input strings) and `firstYear: string`. Prefill both once from `useUmkmYear(taxYear)` when it returns a saved row. The request is built from the debounced state with `toClampedDigitString`.
  - Rendering: preview via `useUmkmPreview(request)`, then a table of `result.months`. `tax === null` shows `t.tax.umkm.ineligible`. `exceedsCeiling` shows a `.notice notice--warning`. `t.tax.umkm.noTimeLimitNote` sits in a `.notice notice--info`. `<TaxRuleBadge rules={result.rules}/>`.
  - Save: `useSaveUmkmYear().mutate({ taxYear, monthlyOmzet, firstRegisteredYear })`, which does `PUT /family-groups/:fg/tax/umkm/:year`. Toasts use `t.tax.umkm.saved` / `saveFailed` (with `extractApiErrorMessage`-style handling; move that helper to `_components/api-error.ts` and re-import it in `page.tsx`).
- **`FinalIncomePanel`.**
  - Data: `useFinalTax(taxYear)`.
  - Columns: source badge (`badge--info` Otomatis / neutral Manual), instrument name (`t.tax.final.instruments[line.instrument]`) with the line label under it in muted text, gross, `formatBps(rateBps)`, tax (`—` when `null`, plus `t.tax.final.missingRate` in `text-warning`), caveat text, `<TaxRuleBadge rules={[line.rule]} />`, and actions (manual lines only, `canManage`).
  - Footer: total plus the missing-rate and skipped-foreign notices.
  - Edit and delete use `useFinalIncomeMutations(taxYear)`. Delete confirms via `ConfirmDeleteDialog` from `@/components/crud`.
- **`FinalIncomeDialog`.**
  - Uses `Dialog` from `@/components/ui/dialog` (import only).
  - The instrument select lists the manual instruments: `dividen`, `bunga_tabungan`, `sewa`, `p2p`, `emas`, `kupon_sbn`, `bunga_deposito`.
  - Fields: gross (`.input` digits), date (`<input type="date">`), and a reinvested checkbox rendered only for `dividen`.
  - Rate override: the percent string is converted to bps with `Math.round(Number(v.replace(",", ".")) * 100)`; empty → `null`. When the chosen instrument's rule has no rate (`emas`, `p2p`), the hint says it is required, but the server accepts empty and shows "—".
  - Notes: max 200 characters.
- **`AnnualSummary`.**
  - Data: `useTaxSummary(taxYear)`.
  - Big total (`t.tax.summary.totalLabel` with `{year}`), then three supporting figures. PPh OP shows its balance line: positive → `balanceDue`, negative → `balanceOver` in `text-success`.
  - SPT row: status label (existing `t.tax.sptStatusLabels`), deadline date, `daysLeft`/`daysLate`, `graceNote` when `graceUntil` is non-null, `filedAtPrefix` + date when `filedAt` is set, and `coretaxNote` as muted text.
  - `<TaxRuleBadge rules={summary.rules}/>`.

Mount in `page.tsx`:

- `<AnnualSummary taxYear={taxYear} />` directly after the three SPT-status cards.
- `<SectionCard id="pajak-final" title={t.tax.final.title}><FinalIncomePanel taxYear={taxYear} canManage={canManageTaxRecords} /></SectionCard>` and `<SectionCard title={t.tax.umkm.title}><UmkmPanel …/></SectionCard>` after the TER panel.

If `SectionCard` does not forward `id`, wrap it in `<section id="pajak-final">` (do not edit `components/wm`).

### Task 11: Web surfaces — /investasi, /rekening, /laporan, /transaksi (PPN info)

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/web/src/components/tax/investment-tax-card.tsx` (+`.test.tsx`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/tax/account-tax-notice.tsx` (+`.test.tsx`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/tax/ppn-hint.tsx` (+`.test.tsx`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(app)/laporan/_components/sections/pajak-final-card.tsx`
- Modify (one mount each, see the concurrency rule): `apps/web/src/app/(app)/investasi/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(app)/rekening/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(app)/transaksi/page.tsx` (inside `TransactionDetailDialog`), `apps/web/src/app/(app)/laporan/_components/sections/pajak-section.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/i18n/types.ts`, `id.ts`, `en.ts` (`tax.invest`, `tax.account`, `tax.ppn`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `useInvestmentTaxEstimate`, `useSetCryptoExchange`, `useTaxRules`, `usePpnPreview`, `TaxRuleBadge`, `formatBps`, `useMyAccess` (`@/hooks/use-my-access`, read-only), contract `FinalTaxEstimateDto`, `TaxRuleDetailDto` (Tasks 5–6), `PpnPreviewResultDto`, `TransactionDto`.
- Produces:
  - `<InvestmentTaxCard />`: current WIB tax year. Total estimated final tax from holdings, top 5 lines, a per-line exchange select for kripto lines, `<TaxRuleBadge>`, and a link "Lihat di Pajak" to `/tax#pajak-final` shown only when the viewer can open `tax`.
  - `<AccountTaxNotice />`: the savings/deposit interest rule, read from `GET /calculators/tax-rules?keys=final.savingsInterest,final.depositoInterest` with values. The text is built from `value.rateBps` and `value.minPrincipal`, never hardcoded.
  - `<PpnHint amount={string} occurredAt={string} />`: collapsed `<details>` "Info PPN". It shows DPP, PPN and the effective rate for the price, treated as **including** PPN (receipts include it), with a "barang mewah" toggle and the label "hanya informasi — tidak mengubah transaksi".

`GET /calculators/tax-rules` already returns `TaxRuleDetailDto[]` (ref + `value`, BigInt as digit strings) since Task 6. `AccountTaxNotice` narrows `value` with a type guard (`typeof v === "object" && v !== null && "rateBps" in v`) and renders nothing if the shape is unexpected.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the three components + laporan card**

- `ppn-hint.tsx`:
  - Local helpers: `const wibDate = (iso: string) => new Date(new Date(iso).getTime() + 7 * 3_600_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)` and `const digits = (amount: string) => amount.split(".")[0]!.replace(/\D/g, "") || "0"`.

**Placeholder scan:** no TBD/TODO. Every test named in a task has code or an exact assertion list, and every production file has either full code or a binding list of behaviours with exact names.

**Type consistency:**

- `FinalInstrument` = Prisma `TaxFinalInstrument` = contract `TaxFinalInstrument`; parity is enforced by `prisma-enum-parity.spec.ts`.
- `TaxRuleRef` (API) mirrors `TaxRuleRefDto` field-for-field (the mapper spreads it).
- `terYear` returns `TerYearResult` → `toTerResultDto` → `TerPreviewResultDto`.
- `umkmYear` → `toUmkmResultDto(r, taxYear, firstRegisteredYear, saved)`.
- `computePphOp` → `toPphOpResultDto`.
- Hook names in Task 9's Interfaces are the ones Tasks 10–11 consume.

**Rounding rule check (research):** the research note does not state PKP rounding. The plan keeps the existing floor-to-Rp 1.000 as a labelled, unverified rule rather than inventing or dropping it.

## Open questions for the user

1. **TER band tables.** The research gives only the category endpoints. The 125 band values in `pph21.rules.ts` are transcribed from the PP 58/2023 Lampiran, match every endpoint the research gives, and ship labelled "perkiraan". Should someone verify them against the PDF before release so the label can be dropped?
2. **Tax years before 2022.** Existing records for 2021 and earlier keep using the UU HPP brackets (unchanged numbers, now labelled "perkiraan"). Is that acceptable, or should the pre-HPP brackets (50/250/500 jt, top 30 %) be modelled? Modelling them would change those old records.

## Appendix A — golden-number script (planning-time verification)

Independent BigInt reference for every golden number in this plan. It also includes a byte-for-byte copy of the pre-L float formula for the legacy rows and the float-vs-exact biaya jabatan sweep. Run: `node .superpowers/sdd/2026-09-26-revisi-l-pajak-2026/golden-l.mjs`.

```js
// Independent reference for revisi L golden numbers (BigInt, round-half-up).
const J = 1_000_000n;
const div = (n, d) => {
  const q = n / d,
    r = n % d;
  return 2n * r >= d ? q + 1n : q;
}; // non-negative only
const bps = (amt, b) => div(amt * BigInt(b), 10_000n);
const BR = [
  [60n * J, 500],
  [250n * J, 1500],
  [500n * J, 2500],
  [5000n * J, 3000],
  [null, 3500],
];
function pasal17(pkp) {
  let t = 0n,
    prev = 0n;
  for (const [lim, r] of BR) {
    if (pkp <= prev) break;
    const top = lim === null ? pkp : pkp < lim ? pkp : lim;
    t += ((top - prev) * BigInt(r)) / 10000n;
    prev = lim ?? pkp;
  }
  return t;
}
const ptkp = (st, d) =>
  54n * J +
  (st === "TK" ? 0n : 4_500_000n) +
  (st === "KI" ? 54n * J : 0n) +
  BigInt(d) * 4_500_000n;
const floor1000 = (x) => (x <= 0n ? 0n : (x / 1000n) * 1000n);
function annual({
  gross,
  status,
  dep,
  pension = 0n,
  zakat = 0n,
  employee = true,
  nppnGross = 0n,
  nppnBps = 0,
  other = 0n,
}) {
  // biaya jabatan kept exact in hundredths: min(gross * 5%, 6jt)
  const gross100 = gross * 100n,
    bj100 = employee ? (gross * 5n < 600n * J ? gross * 5n : 600n * J) : 0n;
  const nppnNet = (nppnGross * BigInt(nppnBps)) / 10000n;
  let neto100 =
    gross100 - bj100 - pension * 100n + nppnNet * 100n + other * 100n;
  if (neto100 < 0n) neto100 = 0n;
  const zak100 = zakat * 100n > neto100 ? neto100 : zakat * 100n;
  const pkp = floor1000((neto100 - zak100 - ptkp(status, dep) * 100n) / 100n);
  return {
    bj: bj100 / 100n,
    bj100,
    pkp,
    tax: pasal17(pkp),
    ptkp: ptkp(status, dep),
  };
}

// Legacy float formula (byte-for-byte copy of pph21.util.ts before revisi L) -> characterization rows
const FBR = [
  [60e6, 0.05],
  [250e6, 0.15],
  [500e6, 0.25],
  [5e9, 0.3],
  [Infinity, 0.35],
];
function legacy(g, p, z) {
  const bj = Math.min(g * 0.05, 6e6);
  const pkp = Math.floor(Math.max(0, g - bj - p - z) / 1000) * 1000;
  let rem = pkp,
    prev = 0,
    t = 0;
  for (const [l, r] of FBR) {
    if (rem <= 0) break;
    const w = l === Infinity ? rem : Math.min(rem, l - prev);
    const ta = Math.min(rem, w);
    t += ta * r;
    rem -= ta;
    prev = l;
  }
  return Math.round(t);
}
for (const [g, p, z] of [

  [120e6, 63e6, 2.5e6],
  [123456789, 58.5e6, 1234567],
  [180e6, 72e6, 0],
  [250e6, 54e6, 10e6],
  [350e6, 67.5e6, 0],
  [600e6, 54e6, 0],
  [1e9, 58.5e6, 25e6],
  [6e9, 72e6, 0],
  [999999999999999, 54e6, 0],
  [50e6, 54e6, 100e6],
])
  console.log("legacy", g, p, z, "->", legacy(g, p, z));
// Float-vs-exact biaya jabatan check over every multiple of Rp20 up to Rp200jt
{
  let bad = 0;
  for (let g = 0; g <= 200_000_000; g += 20) {
    const f = Math.min(g * 0.05, 6e6),
      e = g >= 120e6 ? 6e6 : g / 20;
    if (f !== e) bad++;
  }
  console.log("bj float mismatches", bad);
}
```
